     8	/**
     9	 * The template surface, composed: ONE engine run over the declaration on
    10	 * whichever channel the caller is on, plus the AUDITED RESIDUAL — the
    11	 * lanes that ADR-019 D1 leaves to prose/code — wired into the SAME finding
    12	 * pipeline so the output is one uniform stream.
    13	 *
    14	 * The residual realized here, by audit family:
    15	 *
    16	 * - **R3** the existential cross-rule over resolved registrations
    17	 *   (ch11-C19 → ch12-C5): "IF any binding resolves to a registration whose
    18	 *   `requiresRuntimeContext` is true THEN `$.runtimeContext` must be a
    19	 *   spec map" is a conditional over a DERIVED property of an injected
    20	 *   object; a `when:` general enough to express it is an expression
    21	 *   language.
    22	 * - **R7** the uses-scoped SOURCE ladder on two gate-config integers
    23	 *   (ch11-C12's source half). Its declaration exists — `[vc-authored-int]`
    24	 *   — but realizing it inside the engine requires the `delegate` hand-off
    25	 *   to carry the CHANNEL into the registration, which is a change to the
    26	 *   ratified `GateRegistration` port shape: out of ADR-019 D2's scope and
    27	 *   exactly the "while we are here" D6 forbids. Under D7's ≥2-row test the
    28	 *   alternative — a construct that scopes a source lane by a sibling's
    29	 *   value, serving this one row — is refused, and the row keeps a code
    30	 *   lane. This is R7's first live member; the family was kept open at the
    31	 *   ratification for precisely this case.
    32	 *
    33	 * Not here, and deliberately: **R5** (the store's declared-ref/filename
    34	 * check) stays the store stage's, and **R6** is library-owned wording.
    35	 */
    36	
    37	export interface SurfaceOutcome {
    38	  readonly findings: readonly ValidationFinding[];
    39	  /** The admitted VALUE, unbranded: ch11-P2a A6 keeps `admitTemplate` the
    40	   * ONLY sanctioned producer of the `AdmittedTemplate` brand, and that
    41	   * guard is lint-enforced. The engine computes the value; admission
    42	   * brands it. */
    43	  readonly admitted?: unknown;
    44	}
    45	
    46	const RUNTIME_CONTEXT_CODE = "runtime_context_required_for_process_gate";
    47	
    48	/** R7: the two uses-scoped integer fields whose SOURCE form is laddered by
    49	 * the walk. The scoping is SYNTACTIC — the authored `uses` string, no
    50	 * catalog resolution — exactly as the measured lane does it. */
    51	const SOURCE_SCOPED_CONFIG_FIELDS: Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>> = {
    52	  "declarative.threshold": ["value"],
    53	  "external.process": ["timeoutMs"],
    54	};
    55	
    56	function isContainer(value: unknown): value is ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown> {
    57	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    58	}
    59	
    60	function entriesOf(
    61	  value: ReadonlyMap<unknown, unknown> | Record<string, unknown>,
    62	): readonly (readonly [unknown, unknown])[] {
    63	  if (value instanceof Map) return [...value.entries()];
    64	  return Object.keys(value).map((key) => [key, (value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]] as const);
    65	}
    66	
    67	function get(value: unknown, key: string): unknown {
    68	  if (!isContainer(value)) return undefined;
    69	  if (value instanceof Map) return value.get(key);
    70	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key) ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)[key] : undefined;
    71	}
    72	
    73	/** R7's walk: `steps.<id>.gates.<event>[<i>].config.<field>` for the two
    74	 * scoped fields, on the file channel only (no source, no lane). */
    75	function sourceScopedFindings(value: unknown, channel: EngineChannel): ValidationFinding[] {
    76	  if (channel.kind !== "file") return [];
    77	  const findings: ValidationFinding[] = [];
    78	  const steps = get(value, "steps");
    79	  if (!isContainer(steps)) return findings;
    80	  for (const [stepId, step] of entriesOf(steps)) {
    81	    if (typeof stepId !== "string" || !isContainer(step)) continue;
    82	    const gates = get(step, "gates");
    83	    if (!isContainer(gates)) continue;
    84	    for (const [eventType, pipeline] of entriesOf(gates)) {
    85	      if (typeof eventType !== "string" || !Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
    86	      pipeline.forEach((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
    87	        if (!isContainer(binding)) return;
    88	        const uses = get(binding, "uses");
    89	        const config = get(binding, "config");
    90	        if (typeof uses !== "string" || !isContainer(config)) return;
    91	        for (const field of SOURCE_SCOPED_CONFIG_FIELDS[uses] ?? []) {
    92	          if (get(config, field) === undefined) continue;
    93	          const path = `steps.${stepId}.gates.${eventType}[${String(index)}].config.${field}`;
    94	          const node: unknown = channel.doc.getIn(astPath(path), true);
    95	          const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, channel.source, path);
    96	          if (message !== undefined) findings.push({ path, message });
    97	        }
    98	      });
    99	    }
   100	  }
   101	  return findings;
   102	}
   103	
   104	/**
   105	 * R3: exactly ONE template-grain finding when a resolved process gate
   106	 * demands a runtime context the template does not provision. The lane is
   107	 * a DEPENDENT of the `runtimeContext` node: an illegal value fired its own
   108	 * container finding and left the normalized field absent, which suppresses
   109	 * this one (ch8-C21's container-precondition rule).
   110	 */
   111	function runtimeContextCrossRule(
   112	  normalized: unknown,
   113	  bindings: readonly string[],
   114	): ValidationFinding | undefined {
   115	  if (bindings.length === 0) return undefined;
   116	  const requirement = get(normalized, "runtimeContext");
   117	  if (requirement === undefined || isContainer(requirement)) return undefined;
   118	  return {
   119	    path: "runtimeContext",
   120	    code: RUNTIME_CONTEXT_CODE,
   121	    message:
   122	      `template declares process gate(s) requiring runtime context but does not ` +
   123	      `declare a provisionable runtimeContext spec { kind, provider } (gates: ${bindings.join(", ")})`,
   124	  };
   125	}
   126	
   127	/**
   128	 * Run the template surface on ONE channel. All-or-nothing: any finding
   129	 * means no admitted value exists (ch8-C22), and the admitted form is
   130	 * produced by the NORMALIZER, never by the validator (D3).
   131	 */
   132	export function runTemplateSurface(
   133	  value: unknown,
   134	  channel: EngineChannel,
   135	  catalog: GateCatalog,
   136	): SurfaceOutcome {
   137	  const run = runSurface(templateFormat, value, { channel, catalog });
   138	  const findings: ValidationFinding[] = [...run.findings];
   139	  findings.push(...sourceScopedFindings(value, channel));
   140	  const crossRule = runtimeContextCrossRule(run.normalized, run.runtimeContextBindings);
   141	  if (crossRule !== undefined) findings.push(crossRule);
   142	  if (findings.length > 0) return { findings };
   143	  return { findings: [], admitted: normalize(templateFormat, run.normalized, run.effectiveConfigs) };
   144	}

     4	import type { GateCatalog } from "../ports/index.js";
     5	import { admitFromSource } from "./admit.js";
     6	import type { PipelineFinding, TemplateLoadResult, ValidationFinding } from "./errors.js";
     7	import { renderMessage } from "./schema/engine.js";
     8	import { templateFormat } from "./schema/templateFormat.js";
     9	
    10	/**
    11	 * Packet ch8-P1 (G1/draft C36): the staged load pipeline over raw
    12	 * bytes — read (strict UTF-8 decode) → parse (the document step AND
    13	 * the directive check) → resolve (`toJS`, where the alias-amplification
    14	 * guard fires) → validate. Each stage SHORT-CIRCUITS: the first
    15	 * failing stage's finding(s) are the entire result; every stage's
    16	 * unexpected throw is caught and mapped (no load input produces an
    17	 * uncaught throw — E3/draft C22 binds every stage). The OS half of
    18	 * the read stage belongs to the CALLER that reads files (the store;
    19	 * P2's dev validate) — `opts.path` stamps read-stage findings per
    20	 * E5's presence scoping.
    21	 */
    22	export interface LoadTemplateOptions {
    23	  readonly path?: string;
    24	  /**
    25	   * The gate catalog admission resolves `uses` against (ch11-P2a, A1).
    26	   * OPTIONAL: absent it defaults to the empty catalog — vacuous over a
    27	   * gate-free template (every `uses` resolves unknown). Since ch11-P4 a
    28	   * loadable YAML CAN author `gates`, so a file with gate bindings needs
    29	   * the real catalog to admit; the empty-catalog default stays exact for
    30	   * gate-free files. File callers (the store, the dev validate verb) pass
    31	   * the real catalog.
    32	   */
    33	  readonly catalog?: GateCatalog;
    34	}
    35	
    36	/** The empty catalog: every `uses` is unknown. Vacuous over zero
    37	 * bindings (every gate-free template) — A8's byte-identical default. */
    38	const EMPTY_CATALOG: GateCatalog = { resolve: () => null };
    39	
    40	/** ADR-019 D4: the substrate stage's wording and its one version knob are
    41	 * DECLARED, so editing the declaration edits this pipeline's behaviour.
    42	 * What stays here is the yaml library's own behaviour, which no
    43	 * declaration can move (audit residual R6). */
    44	const SUBSTRATE = templateFormat.substrate;
    45	
    46	function fail(stage: "read" | "parse" | "resolve", findings: readonly PipelineFinding[]): TemplateLoadResult;
    47	function fail(stage: "validate", findings: readonly ValidationFinding[]): TemplateLoadResult;
    48	function fail(
    49	  stage: "read" | "parse" | "resolve" | "validate",
    50	  findings: readonly PipelineFinding[] | readonly ValidationFinding[],
    51	): TemplateLoadResult {
    52	  return { ok: false, error: { stage, findings } };
    53	}
    54	
    55	function errorMessage(error: unknown): string {
    56	  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
    57	}
    58	
    59	function nodeAnchor(value: unknown): string | undefined {
    60	  if (!(isScalar(value) || isMap(value) || isSeq(value)) || !("anchor" in value)) return undefined;
    61	  return typeof value.anchor === "string" ? value.anchor : undefined;
    62	}
    63	
    64	type ComparedNodePairs = WeakMap<object, WeakSet<object>>;
    65	
    66	function markCompared(a: unknown, b: unknown, compared: ComparedNodePairs): boolean {
    67	  if (typeof a !== "object" || a === null || typeof b !== "object" || b === null) return false;
    68	  const matches = compared.get(a);
    69	  if (matches?.has(b) === true) return true;
    70	  if (matches) matches.add(b);
    71	  else compared.set(a, new WeakSet([b]));
    72	  return false;
    73	}
    74	
    75	function resolveAlias(value: unknown, doc: Document | undefined): unknown {
    76	  return doc && isAlias(value) ? (value.resolve(doc) ?? value) : value;
    77	}
    78	
    79	function sameValueZero(a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean {
    80	  return a === b || (a !== a && b !== b);
    81	}
    82	
    83	function yamlNodeEqual(
    84	  left: unknown,
    85	  right: unknown,
    86	  doc?: Document,
    87	  compared: ComparedNodePairs = new WeakMap(),
    88	): boolean {
    89	  const a = resolveAlias(left, doc);
    90	  const b = resolveAlias(right, doc);
    91	  if (a === b) return true;
    92	  if (markCompared(a, b, compared)) return true;
    93	  // SameValueZero, not Object.is: the comparator's equality must be at
    94	  // least as coarse as the downstream Map's KEY identity (SameValueZero),
    95	  // or a distinct-judged pair collapses silently after the gate — the
    96	  // integration re-check's 0/-0 catch (Object.is(0, -0) is false, the
    97	  // Map still merges them). YAML canonical int equality also makes
    98	  // 0 == -0; NaN == NaN holds under both forms.
    99	  if (isScalar(a) && isScalar(b)) return sameValueZero(a.value, b.value);
   100	  if (isAlias(a) && isAlias(b)) return a.source === b.source;
   101	  if (isAlias(a) && nodeAnchor(b) !== undefined) return nodeAnchor(b) === a.source;
   102	  if (nodeAnchor(a) !== undefined && isAlias(b)) return nodeAnchor(a) === b.source;
   103	  if (isPair(a) && isPair(b)) {
   104	    return yamlNodeEqual(a.key, b.key, doc, compared) && yamlNodeEqual(a.value, b.value, doc, compared);
   105	  }
   106	  if (isSeq(a) && isSeq(b)) {
   107	    return a.items.length === b.items.length
   108	      && a.items.every((item, index) => yamlNodeEqual(item, b.items[index], doc, compared));
   109	  }
   110	  if (isMap(a) && isMap(b)) {
   111	    if (a.items.length !== b.items.length) return false;
   112	    const matched = new Set<number>();
   113	    return a.items.every((pair) => {
   114	      const index = b.items.findIndex((candidate, candidateIndex) => (
   115	        !matched.has(candidateIndex) && yamlNodeEqual(pair, candidate, doc, compared)
   116	      ));
   117	      if (index < 0) return false;
   118	      matched.add(index);
   119	      return true;
   120	    });
   121	  }
   122	  return false;
   123	}
   124	
   125	interface PositionedFinding {
   126	  readonly offset: number;
   127	  readonly finding: PipelineFinding;
   128	}
   129	
   130	function resolvedDuplicateFindings(doc: Document, lineCounter: LineCounter): readonly PositionedFinding[] {
   131	  const findings: PositionedFinding[] = [];
   132	  const visit = (node: unknown): void => {
   133	    if (isMap(node)) {
   134	      for (let right = 1; right < node.items.length; right += 1) {
   135	        const rightKey = node.items[right]?.key;
   136	        const earlierKeys = node.items.slice(0, right).map((pair) => pair.key);
   137	        const hasComposeTimeMatch = earlierKeys.some((leftKey) => yamlNodeEqual(leftKey, rightKey));
   138	        const hasResolvedMatch = !hasComposeTimeMatch
   139	          && earlierKeys.some((leftKey) => yamlNodeEqual(leftKey, rightKey, doc));
   140	        if (!hasResolvedMatch) continue;
   141	
   142	        const range = (rightKey as { readonly range?: readonly number[] } | null)?.range;
   143	        const offset = range?.[0] ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
   144	        const pos = Number.isSafeInteger(offset) ? lineCounter.linePos(offset) : undefined;
   145	        findings.push({
   146	          offset,
   147	          finding: {
   148	            stage: "parse",
   149	            ...(pos && pos.line > 0 ? { line: pos.line, col: pos.col } : {}),
   150	            message: SUBSTRATE.parse.duplicateKeyMessage,
   151	          },
   152	        });
   153	      }
   154	      for (const pair of node.items) visit(pair);
   155	    } else if (isSeq(node)) {
   156	      for (const item of node.items) visit(item);
   157	    } else if (isPair(node)) {
   158	      visit(node.key);
   159	      visit(node.value);
   160	    }
   161	  };
   162	  visit(doc.contents);
   163	  return findings;
   164	}
   165	
   166	/** E1 (draft C20): positional parse findings — 1-based line/col where the parser provides them. */
   167	function parseFinding(error: YAMLError): PipelineFinding {
   168	  const pos = error.linePos?.[0];
   169	  return {
   170	    stage: "parse",
   171	    ...(pos ? { line: pos.line, col: pos.col } : {}),
   172	    message: error.message,
   173	  };
   174	}
   175	
   176	export function loadTemplate(bytes: Uint8Array, opts: LoadTemplateOptions = {}): TemplateLoadResult {
   177	  // READ stage, decode half (G3/draft C6): strict UTF-8 — invalid bytes
   178	  // reject with a content-free message, never the silent U+FFFD repair.
   179	  let text: string;
   180	  try {
   181	    text = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(bytes);
   182	  } catch {
   183	    return fail("read", [
   184	      {
   185	        stage: "read",
   186	        ...(opts.path !== undefined ? { path: opts.path } : {}),
   187	        message: SUBSTRATE.read.message,
   188	      },
   189	    ]);
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  // PARSE stage (G4–G7/draft C2–C4, C34): the document API with BOTH
   193	  // errors AND warnings promoted (fail-closed), plus the directive
   194	  // check — its synthesized finding HEADS the ordered list.
   195	  let doc: ReturnType<typeof parseDocument>;
   196	  const lineCounter = new LineCounter();
   197	  try {
   198	    doc = parseDocument(text, {
   199	      lineCounter,
   200	      uniqueKeys: (a: ParsedNode, b: ParsedNode) => yamlNodeEqual(a, b),
   201	    });
   202	  } catch (error) {
   203	    // Defensive: probes P9/P13 show content diagnostics collect in
   204	    // doc.errors — the stage catch is the C22 every-stage belt.
   205	    return fail("parse", [{ stage: "parse", message: errorMessage(error) }]);
   206	  }
   207	  const parseFindings: PipelineFinding[] = [];
   208	  const yamlDirective = doc.directives?.yaml;
   209	  if (yamlDirective?.explicit === true && yamlDirective.version !== SUBSTRATE.parse.directive.only) {
   210	    // The silently-ADOPTED case (probe P17: %YAML 1.1 — zero errors,
   211	    // zero warnings, the full 1.1 trap restored). Synthesized: the
   212	    // parser emitted nothing, so no position fields exist.
   213	    parseFindings.push({
   214	      stage: "parse",
   215	      message: renderMessage(SUBSTRATE.parse.directive.message, { key: yamlDirective.version }),
   216	    });
   217	  }
   218	  const positionedErrors: PositionedFinding[] = [
   219	    ...doc.errors.map((error) => ({ offset: error.pos[0], finding: parseFinding(error) })),
   220	    ...resolvedDuplicateFindings(doc, lineCounter),
   221	  ].sort((a, b) => a.offset - b.offset);
   222	  for (const { finding } of positionedErrors) {
   223	    parseFindings.push(finding);
   224	  }
   225	  for (const warning of doc.warnings) {
   226	    parseFindings.push(parseFinding(warning));
   227	  }
   228	  if (parseFindings.length > 0) {
   229	    return fail("parse", parseFindings);
   230	  }
   231	
   232	  // RESOLVE stage (G8/draft C5+C36): toJS — the alias-amplification
   233	  // guard throws HERE, invisible to doc.errors (probes P8/P14/P16).
   234	  let value: unknown;
   235	  try {
   236	    // Preserve resolved map-key types through validation. The default
   237	    // object materialization would collapse distinct YAML keys such as
   238	    // `1` and `"1"` before V5 can reject the non-string key.
   239	    value = doc.toJS({ mapAsMap: true });
   240	  } catch (error) {
   241	    return fail("resolve", [{ stage: "resolve", message: errorMessage(error) }]);
   242	  }
   243	
   244	  // VALIDATE stage: ONE run of the template surface's declared schema
   245	  // (ADR-019 D1) over the resolved value graph, on the FILE channel so
   246	  // the source-bearing lanes have an operand. The two rungs this stage
   247	  // used to have — the source-form walk and the admission rung — are one
   248	  // computation over one declaration; their findings were already
   249	  // required to accumulate in ONE `validate` result (ch11-P4 F7), and
   250	  // now they cannot fail to. The result stays XOR (ch8-C22): an admitted
   251	  // template escapes only when the run is findings-free.
   252	  try {
   253	    const admitted = admitFromSource(value, doc, text, opts.catalog ?? EMPTY_CATALOG);
   254	    if (!admitted.ok) {
   255	      return fail("validate", admitted.findings);
   256	    }
   257	    return { ok: true, template: admitted.template };
   258	  } catch (error) {
   259	    // The C22 every-stage belt: no known input reaches this (V15's
   260	    // cycle rule is a FINDING, not a throw), but the stage still maps.
   261	    return fail("validate", [
   262	      {
   263	        path: SUBSTRATE.internalFailure.path,
   264	        message: renderMessage(SUBSTRATE.internalFailure.message, { value: errorMessage(error) }),
   265	      },
   266	    ]);
   267	  }
   268	}
     1	import type { NormalizerHookDecl, SurfaceDecl } from "./vocabulary.js";
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * ADR-019 D3: THE NORMALIZER — the engine's SECOND capability, named so
     5	 * that "schema" is never read as covering it.
     6	 *
     7	 * A declaration says what is LEGAL; it does not compute a value. Admission
     8	 * both validates and PRODUCES the admitted form (the audit's residual R4:
     9	 * `advancesRound` expanded per transition, the effective gate config
    10	 * materialized). Those are TRANSFORMS, and they live here.
    11	 *
    12	 * The boundary is exact and load-bearing:
    13	 * - plain `default:` materialization IS declarable and stays SCHEMA-side
    14	 *   (the engine applies it while walking — `activation`, `runtimeContext`,
    15	 *   `output.mode`, the disposition and reason defaults);
    16	 * - DERIVATION — a value computed from other values — is the normalizer's.
    17	 *
    18	 * Each hook is a NAMED capability parameterized by declared operand paths,
    19	 * so adding one is D7 format growth (a ratified amendment), never an edit
    20	 * that slips in under "normalization".
    21	 */
    22	
    23	/** Own-property WRITE: a step id or event type legally admits `__proto__`. */
    24	function defineOwn<T>(target: Record<string, T>, key: string, value: T): void {
    25	  Object.defineProperty(target, key, { configurable: true, enumerable: true, value, writable: true });
    26	}
    27	
    28	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    29	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    30	}
    31	
    32	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    33	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    34	}
    35	
    36	/** Resolve a declared operand path (`$.steps`, `$.round.advanceOnArrivalAt`)
    37	 * over the normalized value. */
    38	function at(root: unknown, path: string): unknown {
    39	  let cursor: unknown = root;
    40	  for (const segment of path.split(".")) {
    41	    if (segment === "$") continue;
    42	    if (!isRecord(cursor)) return undefined;
    43	    cursor = ownGet(cursor, segment);
    44	  }
    45	  return cursor;
    46	}
    47	
    48	/**
    49	 * R4's first member (ch11-C39): expand each entry's edge map into a
    50	 * COMPLETE per-edge boolean map against the declared advancing set. An
    51	 * absent declaration yields all-false (ch11-C38's none-default); the
    52	 * PRODUCER MONOPOLY holds — the map is recomputed and overwrites whatever
    53	 * the input carried.
    54	 */
    55	function expandFlagMaps(
    56	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    57	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "expandAdvancesRound" }>,
    58	): void {
    59	  const entries = at(root, hook.over);
    60	  if (!isRecord(entries)) return;
    61	  const declared = at(root, hook.advanceSet);
    62	  const advancing = new Set<string>(Array.isArray(declared) ? (declared as string[]) : []);
    63	  for (const key of Object.keys(entries)) {
    64	    const entry = ownGet(entries, key);
    65	    if (!isRecord(entry)) continue;
    66	    const edges = ownGet(entry, hook.edges);
    67	    const flags: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    68	    if (isRecord(edges)) {
    69	      for (const edge of Object.keys(edges)) {
    70	        defineOwn(flags, edge, advancing.has(ownGet(edges, edge) as string));
    71	      }
    72	    }
    73	    defineOwn(entry, hook.into, flags);
    74	  }
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * R4's second member (ch11-C20/C5): write each registration's EFFECTIVE
    79	 * config into the binding's single config surface. The binding is rebuilt
    80	 * from its CARRIED fields plus the effective config — the one downstream
    81	 * config form.
    82	 */
    83	function materializeConfigs(
    84	  root: Record<string, unknown>,
    85	  hook: Extract<NormalizerHookDecl, { hook: "materializeEffectiveConfigs" }>,
    86	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
    87	): void {
    88	  // `over` addresses the pipeline map through two open-map levels:
    89	  // `$.steps.*.gates` — the entry key and the event key are the wildcards.
    90	  const [ownerPath, ...rest] = hook.over.split(".*.");
    91	  const owners = at(root, ownerPath ?? "$");
    92	  if (!isRecord(owners)) return;
    93	  const inner = rest.join(".*.");
    94	  for (const ownerKey of Object.keys(owners)) {
    95	    const owner = ownGet(owners, ownerKey);
    96	    if (!isRecord(owner)) continue;
    97	    const pipelines = ownGet(owner, inner);
    98	    if (!isRecord(pipelines)) continue;
    99	    const rebuiltPipelines: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   100	    for (const eventKey of Object.keys(pipelines)) {
   101	      const pipeline = ownGet(pipelines, eventKey);
   102	      if (!Array.isArray(pipeline)) continue;
   103	      const base = `${ownerPath === "$" ? "" : `${(ownerPath ?? "").replace("$.", "")}.`}${ownerKey}.${inner}.${eventKey}`;
   104	      defineOwn(
   105	        rebuiltPipelines,
   106	        eventKey,
   107	        pipeline.map((binding: unknown, index: number) => {
   108	          const carried: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   109	          if (isRecord(binding)) {
   110	            for (const field of hook.carry) defineOwn(carried, field, ownGet(binding, field));
   111	          }
   112	          defineOwn(carried, hook.into, effectiveConfigs.get(`${base}[${String(index)}]`));
   113	          return carried;
   114	        }),
   115	      );
   116	    }
   117	    defineOwn(owner, inner, rebuiltPipelines);
   118	  }
   119	}
   120	
   121	/**
   122	 * Run every declared normalizer hook over a VALIDATED value. The caller
   123	 * guarantees the value carries no findings — a declaration that refused
   124	 * the input never reaches derivation.
   125	 */
   126	export function normalize(
   127	  surface: SurfaceDecl,
   128	  value: unknown,
   129	  effectiveConfigs: ReadonlyMap<string, unknown>,
   130	): unknown {
   131	  if (!isRecord(value)) return value;
   132	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) {
   133	    if (hook.hook === "expandAdvancesRound") {
   134	      expandFlagMaps(value, hook);
   135	      continue;
   136	    }
   137	    materializeConfigs(value, hook, effectiveConfigs);
   138	  }
   139	  return value;
   140	}

   710	
   711	Of the 70 rows that carry a definition-validation obligation (S+H+Sem),
   712	**61 are fully declarable, 3 are mixed, 6 are residual** — the state
   713	AFTER the ADR-019 ratification (2026-08-05). Before that act it read
   714	59/5/6: ch11-C8 and ch11-C16 were held in R7 precisely so this number
   715	could not borrow against an undecided construct, and D8's ruling
   716	released them. Every one of
   717	the 9 mixed-or-residual rows appears by id as a MEMBER of a residual
   718	family in §4, and no `S` row appears as a member (both directions
   719	checked at rounds 1 and 2 — F5 found two rows missing from every
   720	family, F2 found nine `S` rows sitting in member lists, which is why §4
   721	now separates members from affected rows). Zero rows are unclassified.
   722	
   723	The membership changed at the fold even though the totals did not:
   724	ch8-C16 moved H → S (`dependsOn` covers its suppression), and ch11-C16
   725	moved S → H (its per-member `code:` is single-use, so §0's own rule
   726	forbids banking it). Recording the swap because a stable total across a
   727	fold is exactly where an unchecked reader assumes nothing happened.
   728	
   729	## 4. The residual (named prose/code lanes)
   730	
   731	**Two column meanings, kept apart since round 2 (F2).** A **member** is
   732	a row whose OWN obligation is residual — every member is classified `H`
   733	or `Sem` in §3, and every `H`/`Sem` row is a member of at least one
   734	family. An **affected row** is a row whose obligation IS declarable but
   735	whose text authorizes, or whose findings are shaped by, the residual;
   736	affected rows stay `S`. Mixing the two was making nine `S` rows read as
   737	inexpressible.
   738

   740	|---|---|---|---|
   741	| **R1** | resolution against a value-shaped target | ch13-C7 (entry belt) | C7 requires the target VALUE to satisfy another node's declaration, evaluated at reference time. See §5 F2 — under ONE engine this shrinks to a membership test. |
   742	| **R2** | unreferenced-entry hygiene with a template-wide skip | ch13-C8(c); ch13-C4's skip clause (the ref-container failure that disables the check template-wide) | The set-difference itself IS declarable (`keysSubsetOf: collect(raw: …)`, the same construct `[d-roleset]` uses). What is not: the RAW-member domain (grammar-failing members still count as references) plus the skip-the-whole-check-if-any-ref-container-failed rule. Both are expressible only as a rule-specific predicate — the single-use smell. |
   743	| **R3** | existential cross-rules over resolved registrations | ch11-C19, ch12-C5 (one lane, one code) | "IF any binding resolves to a registration whose `requiresRuntimeContext` is true THEN `$.runtimeContext` must be a spec map" is a conditional over a DERIVED property of an injected object.    750	
   751	Plan §3 (P3) predicted the residual as: *reference resolution,
   752	unreferenced hygiene, event-grain suppression, per-occurrence
   753	duplicates*. The table CHECKED it rather than assuming it:
   754	
   755	| Predicted | Verdict | Basis |
   756	|---|---|---|
   757	| reference resolution | **PARTIALLY REFUTED** | Intra-document references are declarable with one selector vocabulary (`[d-start]`, `[d-target]`, `[d-terminal]` disjointness, `[d-gates]` subset, `[d-round-list]` membership, `[d-roleset]` — 6 independent users). Only the value-shaped belt (R1) and the cross-artifact check (R5) remain. |
   758	| unreferenced hygiene | **PARTIALLY REFUTED** | The set operation is the SAME construct as ch8-C16's role-set equality, which has been shipped and green since ch8. What is residual is C8(c)'s raw-member domain and its template-wide skip, not the hygiene idea. |
   759	| event-grain suppression | **REFUTED** | `keysSubsetOf: keys(../transitions)` + `gating` expresses ch11-C2's dead-config lane and its dependent suppression. Independent ROWS using the construct: 3 — ch8-C16 (whose three tags are one row, corrected at arm F6), ch11-C2, ch11-C15. Three ≥ two, so the verdict stands on the corrected count. |
   760	| per-occurrence duplicates | **REFUTED** | `unique {grain: perOccurrence, at: index\|container}` covers ch8-C17, ch11-C40 and ch13-C8(e) — 3 independent rows. The `at:` attribute exists precisely because the three measured lanes DISAGREE on path grain (§5 F3). |

   770	| **R5** cross-artifact check | A boundary class — the operand is the filesystem, so arguably outside any format schema's remit. Named anyway. |
   771	| **R6** substrate-owned wording | A parity class, not an obligation class (see the table). |

   774	R5 and R6 are boundary/parity classes and could fairly be called out of
   775	the prediction's scope; R3, R4 and R7 could not. The prediction was
   776	made about a surface (ch13) and is being checked against four — that
   777	asymmetry is stated here so the verdicts above are not read as a
   778	scorecard against the plan.

### F3 — A jelenlegi R7 nem az ADR-ben definiált R7

CLASS: design-error  
LENS:  1c  
WHAT: A build az R7 alá sorolta a `ch11-C12` uses-scoped integer source-ladderét, holott az R7 definíciója „nem felvett/single-use vocabulary construct”; itt a construct (`sourceForm: plainDecimalInteger`) felvett és két független sor használja, a valódi ok pedig egy migrációs port-határ.  
WHERE: `v3/adr/ADR-019-declared-schema-for-structural-definition-rules.md:81-87`, `:205-210`; `v3/implementation/schema-expressiveness-audit.md:739-747`; `v3/src/definition/schema/templateSurface.ts:22-31`, `:48-101`  
EVIDENCE: Az audit R7 sora kizárólag „rows whose only declaration uses a single-use construct”, és 2026-08-05-től `RESOLVED EMPTY`. A megvalósítás ezzel szemben ugyanazon név első live memberének mond egy kézzel írt, `uses`-érték alapján elágazó file-channel walkot. Saját kommentje szerint a `sourceForm` deklaráció létezik, és azért nem kerülhet az engine-be, mert a delegated registration nem kapja meg a channelt. Ez nem vocabulary-expressiveness kudarc, hanem a jelenlegi engine/port kompozíciós határának hiánya.  
WHY IT MATTERS: A „residual pontosan hét auditált család” falszifikálhatósági korlátja úgy marad látszólag zöld, hogy egy új maradéktípust régi azonosító alá neveztek át; emiatt a D9.3 tripwire nem jelzi azt az esetet, amelyre létrehozták.

   525	const DEFAULT_MISSING = 'missing required key "{key}"';
   526	
   527	/** Sibling reliability inside ONE fixed-map evaluation. `dependsOn` reads
   528	 * it before the global map so a defect in binding #1 cannot suppress a
   529	 * lane in binding #2. */
   530	type LocalOk = Map<string, boolean>;
   531	
   532	function baseSlots(frame: Frame): Slots {
   533	  return { path: frame.path, value: frame.value, ownerKey: frame.ownerKey, label: frame.label };
   534	}
   535	
   536	function evaluateNode(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   537	  const result = dispatch(run, decl, frame, local);
   538	  run.completed.add(frame.decl);
   539	  run.mark(frame.decl, result.ok);
   540	  run.mark(decl.tag, result.ok);
   541	  return result;
   542	}
   543	
   544	function dispatch(run: Run, decl: NodeDecl, frame: Frame, local?: LocalOk): NodeResult {
   545	  switch (decl.kind) {
   546	    case "map.fixed":
   547	      return evalMapFixed(run, decl, frame);
   548	    case "map.open":
   549	      return evalMapOpen(run, decl, frame);
   550	    case "map.plain":
   551	      return evalMapPlain(run, decl, frame);
   552	    case "list":
   553	      return evalList(run, decl, frame, local);
   554	    case "string":
   555	      return evalString(run, decl, frame, local);
   556	    case "integer":
   557	      return evalInteger(run, decl, frame);
   558	    case "enum":
   559	      return evalEnum(run, decl, frame);
   560	    case "union":
   561	      return evalUnion(run, decl, frame);
   562	    case "raw":
   563	      return evalRaw(run, decl, frame);
   564	    case "valueClass":
   565	      return evalValueClassRef(run, decl, frame, local);
   566	    case "delegate":
   567	      return evalDelegate(run, decl, frame, local);
   568	  }
   569	}
   570	
   571	function evalValueClassRef(
   572	  run: Run,
   573	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "valueClass" }>,
   574	  frame: Frame,
   575	  local?: LocalOk,
   576	): NodeResult {
   577	  const target = run.valueClasses[decl.valueClass];
   578	  if (target === undefined) return { ok: true, value: frame.value };
   579	  const merged: Frame = { ...frame, label: decl.label ?? frame.label };
   580	  const result = dispatch(run, target, merged, local);
   581	  // The REFERENCING site's gating decides, not the shared class's.
   582	  if (!result.ok && decl.gating === true) run.mark(frame.decl, false);
   583	  return result;
   584	}
   585	
   586	function evalMapFixed(
   587	  run: Run,
   588	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "map.fixed" }>,
   589	  frame: Frame,
   590	): NodeResult {
   591	  const value = frame.value;
   592	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   593	  if (!isContainer(value)) {
   594	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.containerMessage, slots));
   595	    return { ok: false };
   596	  }
   597	  const container = value;
   598	  const fieldNames = Object.keys(decl.fields);
   599	  // A field declared for ONE channel is legal only there — the same
   600	  // channel scoping D1 applies to source-bearing attributes, applied to
   601	  // keyset membership.
   602	  const legal = new Set(
   603	    fieldNames.filter((name) => {
   604	      const channel = decl.fields[name]?.channel;
   605	      return channel === undefined || channel === "both" || channel === run.channel.kind;
   606	    }),
   607	  );
   608	  const normalized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   609	  const local: LocalOk = new Map();
   610	
   611	  const emitUnknownKeys = (): void => {
   612	    for (const [key] of entriesOf(container)) {
   613	      if (typeof key !== "string") {
   614	        run.emit(
   615	          frame.path,
   616	          render(decl.unknownMessage, { ...slots, key, value: key, keys: fieldNames.join(", ") }),
   617	        );
   618	        continue;
   619	      }
   620	      if (legal.has(key)) continue;
   621	      const at = joinPath(frame.path, key);
   622	      const removed = decl.removedKeys?.[key];
   623	      const keySlots: Slots = { ...slots, path: at, key, value: key, keys: fieldNames.join(", ") };
   624	      run.emit(at, render(removed ?? decl.unknownMessage, keySlots));
   625	    }
   626	  };
   627	
   628	  const emitMissing = (name: string): void => {
   629	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   630	    const presence = field?.presence;
   631	    if (field === undefined || presence?.required !== true) return;
   632	    if (containerHas(container, name)) return;
   633	    if (presence.foldedIntoTypeLane === true) {
   634	      // Absence is this node's OWN type lane (a binding's `uses`).
   635	      evaluateNode(run, field, childFrame(frame, name, undefined, container), local);
   636	      local.set(field.tag, false);
   637	      return;
   638	    }
   639	    const at = presence.at === "self" ? joinPath(frame.path, name) : frame.path;
   640	    run.emit(
   641	      at,
   642	      render(presence.message ?? decl.missingMessage ?? DEFAULT_MISSING, { ...slots, path: at, key: name }),
   643	      presence.code,
   644	    );
   645	    local.set(field.tag, false);
   646	  };
   647	
   648	  const evalOneField = (name: string): void => {
   649	    const field = decl.fields[name];
   650	    if (field === undefined) return;
   651	    if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   652	      // An ABSENT field is where a declared `default:` materializes — the
   653	      // schema-side half of ADR-019 D3.
   654	      if (field.default !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, field.default);
   655	      return;
   656	    }
   657	    const child = childFrame(frame, name, containerGet(container, name), container);
   658	    const result = evaluateNode(run, field, child, local);
   659	    local.set(field.tag, result.ok);
   660	    if (result.ok && result.value !== undefined) defineOwn(normalized, name, result.value);
   661	  };
   662	
   663	  const order = decl.laneOrder ?? "unknownThenPerKey";
   664	  if (order === "missingThenUnknown") {
   665	    for (const name of fieldNames) emitMissing(name);
   666	    emitUnknownKeys();
   667	    for (const name of fieldNames) evalOneField(name);
   668	  } else if (order === "unknownThenMissingThenValues") {
   669	    emitUnknownKeys();
   670	    for (const name of fieldNames) emitMissing(name);
   671	    for (const name of fieldNames) evalOneField(name);
   672	  } else {
   673	    emitUnknownKeys();
   674	    for (const name of fieldNames) {
   675	      if (!containerHas(container, name)) {
   676	        emitMissing(name);
   677	        evalOneField(name);
   678	        continue;
   679	      }
   680	      evalOneField(name);
   681	    }
   682	  }
   683	  return { ok: true, value: normalized };
   684	}
   685	
   686	function childFrame(parent: Frame, key: string, value: unknown, parentValue: unknown): Frame {
   687	  return {
   688	    decl: `${parent.decl}.${key}`,
   689	    path: joinPath(parent.path, key),
   690	    value,
   900	  const value = frame.value;
   901	  if (decl.sourceForm === "plainDecimalInteger" && run.channel.kind === "file") {
   902	    const node: unknown = run.channel.doc.getIn(astPath(frame.path), true);
   903	    const message = sourceLadderFinding(node, run.channel.source, frame.path);
   904	    if (message !== undefined) {
   905	      run.emit(frame.path, message);
   906	      return { ok: false };
   907	    }
   908	    return { ok: true, value };
   909	  }
   910	  const belt = decl.resolvedForm;
   911	  if (belt?.safeInteger === true) {
   912	    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < belt.min) {
   913	      run.emit(frame.path, render(belt.message, baseSlots(frame)), decl.presence?.code);
   914	      return { ok: false };
   915	    }
   916	  }
   917	  return { ok: true, value };
   918	}
   919	
   920	function evalEnum(
   921	  run: Run,
   922	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "enum" }>,
   923	  frame: Frame,
   924	): NodeResult {
   925	  const value = frame.value;
   926	  const legal = decl.members.filter(
   927	    (one) => one.channel === undefined || one.channel === "both" || one.channel === run.channel.kind,
   928	  );
   929	  const slots: Slots = {
   930	    ...baseSlots(frame),
   931	    members: legal.map((one) => String(one.value)).join(", "),
   932	  };
   933	  const member = legal.find((one) => one.value === value);
   934	  if (member !== undefined) return { ok: true, value: member.store ?? member.value };
   935	  run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.message, slots), decl.code);
   936	  return { ok: false };
   937	}
   938	
   939	function evalUnion(
   940	  run: Run,
   941	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "union" }>,
   942	  frame: Frame,
   943	): NodeResult {
   944	  const value = frame.value;
   945	  const slots = baseSlots(frame);
   946	  if (typeof value === "string") {
   947	    const removed = decl.removedValues?.[value];
   948	    if (removed !== undefined) {
   949	      run.emit(frame.path, render(removed, slots));
   950	      return { ok: false };
     1	import type { Document } from "yaml";
     2	
     3	import type { AdmittedTemplate, WorkflowTemplate } from "../domain/index.js";
     4	import type { GateCatalog } from "../ports/index.js";
     5	import type { ValidationFinding } from "./errors.js";
     6	import type { EngineChannel } from "./schema/engine.js";
     7	import { runTemplateSurface } from "./schema/templateSurface.js";
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * `admitTemplate`: THE single validation + normalization point for an
    11	 * authored definition (ch11-C20 realized). Since ADR-019 (accepted
    12	 * 2026-08-05) its body is not hand-written rules but ONE run of the
    13	 * template surface's DECLARED SCHEMA — `schema/templateFormat.ts` —
    14	 * through the engine, on whichever channel the caller is on.
    15	 *
    16	 * What that changed, and what it did not:
    17	 *
    18	 * - The FILE pipeline and the direct-construction path now share ONE
    19	 *   computation over ONE declaration (ADR-019 D1). The hand-partitioned
    20	 *   "realization split" that ch11-C40 and ch13-C19 each spent ratified
    21	 *   prose defending is gone: source-bearing attributes are declared once
    22	 *   and run where a source exists. A structurally broken value is refused
    23	 *   on BOTH channels — the parity gate's Class 1 delta, ratified
    24	 *   2026-08-05 (`v3/implementation/p3-parity-gate.md`).
    25	 * - ALL-OR-NOTHING is unchanged (ch8-C22): every lane reports, suppression
    26	 *   stays local to a broken container's dependents, and ANY finding means
    27	 *   no admitted value exists.
    28	 * - The finding form, the issue-code namespace, the path grammar and every
    29	 *   message are unchanged — the parity gate measured zero path deltas and
    30	 *   zero message deltas across 1830 executed cases.
    31	 * - ch11-P2a A6 is unchanged and still lint-guarded: this module is the
    32	 *   ONLY sanctioned producer of the `AdmittedTemplate` brand.
    33	 *
    34	 * NOT here: the admitted FORM is computed by the normalizer (ADR-019 D3),
    35	 * and the audited residual — the runtime-context cross-rule (R3) and the
    36	 * uses-scoped source ladder (R7) — lives in `schema/templateSurface.ts`,
    37	 * wired into this same finding stream.
    38	 */
    39	export type AdmitResult =
    40	  | { readonly ok: true; readonly template: AdmittedTemplate }
    41	  | { readonly ok: false; readonly findings: readonly ValidationFinding[] };
    42	
    43	function admitOn(value: unknown, channel: EngineChannel, catalog: GateCatalog): AdmitResult {
    44	  const outcome = runTemplateSurface(value, channel, catalog);
    45	  if (outcome.admitted === undefined) return { ok: false, findings: outcome.findings };
    46	  // A6: the one sanctioned mint.
    47	  return { ok: true, template: outcome.admitted as AdmittedTemplate };
    48	}
    49	
    50	/** The direct-construction channel: the caller holds a value, not bytes. */
    51	export function admitTemplate(template: WorkflowTemplate, catalog: GateCatalog): AdmitResult {
    52	  return admitOn(template, { kind: "direct" }, catalog);
    53	}
    54	
    55	/**
    56	 * The FILE channel: the resolved value graph plus its source, so the
    57	 * source-bearing lanes (the plain-decimal integer ladder, the gates
    58	 * subtree's key-stringness scan) have an operand. Same declaration, same
    59	 * engine, same findings channel — the load pipeline's validate stage.
    60	 */
    61	export function admitFromSource(
    62	  value: unknown,
    63	  doc: Document,
    64	  source: string,
    65	  catalog: GateCatalog,
    66	): AdmitResult {
    67	  return admitOn(value, { kind: "file", doc, source }, catalog);
    68	}

  1153	/** The declaration's own tag inventory — the operand of D4's tag-closure

  1155	export function collectTags(surface: SurfaceDecl): readonly string[] {

  1159	    switch (decl.kind) {
  1160	      case "map.fixed":
  1161	        for (const field of Object.values(decl.fields)) visit(field);
  1162	        return;
  1163	      case "map.open": {
  1164	        if (decl.keyClass !== undefined) visit(decl.keyClass);
  1165	        visit(decl.entry);
  1166	        return;
  1167	      }
  1168	      case "list":
  1169	        visit(decl.member);
  1170	        return;
  1171	      case "union":
  1172	        if (decl.mapCase !== undefined) visit(decl.mapCase);
  1173	        return;
  1174	      default:
  1175	        return;
  1176	    }
  1177	  };

  1181	  for (const hook of surface.normalizers) tags.push(hook.tag);
  1182	  return tags;

### F6 — A következő contract nem tud minden deklarált obligationre taggel hivatkozni

CLASS: future-trap  
LENS:  2a  
WHAT: A node/cross-rule/normalizer rész 43 címezhető taget ad, de a `substrate` öt ága egyetlen taget és `rows` kapcsolatot sem hordoz, ezért a P4 contract-author nem tudja a D4 szerinti tag-pointerrel megnevezni többek között a decode, YAML-directive, duplicate-key, cycle és internal-failure deklarációt.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:350-383`; `v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:479-506`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:1153-1182`; `v3/adr/ADR-019-declared-schema-for-structural-definition-rules.md:111-138`, `:291-298`  
EVIDENCE: A deklaráció bejárása `43` taget és `36` külön row-id-t talál, miközben `templateFormat.substrate` kulcsai `read, parse, resolve, codes, internalFailure`, és a blokkon nincs `tag`. A `collectTags` kizárólag `root`, `valueClasses`, `crossRules`, `normalizers` elemeket jár be, a substrate-ot nem. Konkrétan a ch8-C6 contract-sor a `SUBSTRATE.read.message` authorityre csak fájl/mezőút vagy a szöveg megismétlésével tud mutatni, deklarációs taggel nem.  
WHY IT MATTERS: A P4-ben ígért „decisions plus declaration pointers, restating no attribute” forma nem valósítható meg egységesen a jelenlegi declaration surface fölött, és a hirdetett tag-closure check a teljes substrate hatóságot láthatatlanul hagyja.

### F7 — A `channel` vocabulary többet enged és dokumentál, mint amit az engine értelmez

CLASS: design-error  
LENS:  4a  
WHAT: A `channel` minden `NodeDecl` közös attribútuma és a dokumentáció szerint a node futását/skippelését szabályozza, de az engine csak fixed-map mezők legal-key szűrésénél és enum membereknél olvassa; rooton, list-memberen, open-map entryn, union mapCase-en vagy value-class definíción a mező legális TypeScript, de nincs ilyen jelentése.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:104-115`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:586-681`, `:920-936`  
EVIDENCE:
  `rg -n '\\.channel|channel ===|channel !==' v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts v3/src/definition/schema/normalizer.ts`
  csak a fixed-map field-szűrést (`604-605`), a külön `deepKeyStringness`-t, a `sourceForm`-ot és az enum membereket találja. Konkrét ellenpélda: egy későbbi declaration-author egy list `member` node-jára `channel: "file"`-t ír a NodeBase dokumentációját követve; a típusellenőrzés elfogadja, de a direct csatornán `evalList` ugyanúgy kiértékeli a membert, jelzés nélkül. Még fixed-map fielden is az összes fieldre fut a missing/evaluation loop, nem csak a `legal` halmazra; egy `channel:"file", presence:{required:true}` mező hiányát direct csatornán is jelentené.  
WHY IT MATTERS: A language spec egyik központi attribútuma „honestly misread” módon használható: a deklaráció lefordul, a suite a meglévő öt használattal zöld marad, az új csatorna-szűkítés pedig csendben nem azt jelenti, amit a vocabulary ígér.

     1	import type { GateDecision, GateProjection } from "../domain/index.js";
     2	
     3	/**
     4	 * The gate EXTENSION contract (packet ch11-P2a, R1–R5; ledger §2 l2).
     5	 * The ch-3 placeholder spec/runner shapes are RETIRED (R5) — this port
     6	 * now carries the ledger's registration/catalog descriptor and nothing
     7	 * else. The registry maps a gate's `uses` id to its registration;
     8	 * composition is STATIC (dynamic loading is Absent), injected through
     9	 * the `GateCatalog`.
    10	 */
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * R4: the config-relative finding a registration emits. `path` is
    14	 * relative to the gate's config object (`""` = the config itself, a
    15	 * dotted key otherwise); admission PREFIXES it with the binding's C7
    16	 * address (`steps.<stepId>.gates.<eventType>[<i>].config…`). The
    17	 * message is unconstrained diagnostic text.
    18	 *
    19	 * `code` (packet ch11-P3a, V4/A9) is the named-lane carrier: present on
    20	 * exactly the registration's CODED lanes (`external.process`'s d/e/h/i/k/l
    21	 * → `invalid_process_gate_config`, o → `gate_config_not_supported`),
    22	 * ABSENT on every uncoded lane. Admission propagates it verbatim into the
    23	 * `ValidationFinding` code field.
    24	 */
    25	export interface GateConfigFinding {
    26	  readonly path: string;
    27	  readonly message: string;
    28	  readonly code?: string;
    29	}
    30	
    31	/**
    32	 * R4: the two-arm result of config validation + normalization — the
    33	 * EFFECTIVE config XOR a NONEMPTY finding set (the ledger's
    34	 * `effective | issues`). Defaults materialize on the `ok` arm, once.
    35	 * The failure arm is statically nonempty (arm-gate-2 finding 1): an
    36	 * empty-findings failure would let admission succeed with no effective
    37	 * config — the tuple type forbids it, and admission carries a runtime
    38	 * belt for a cast-forged registration.
    39	 */
    40	export type GateConfigResult =
    41	  | { readonly ok: true; readonly effective: unknown }
    42	  | { readonly ok: false; readonly findings: readonly [GateConfigFinding, ...GateConfigFinding[]] };
    43	
    44	/**
    45	 * R1: the shared descriptor axes. `execution` is realized with its
    46	 * domain pinned to the singleton `"inline"` — `"deferred"` is the
    47	 * model's named Absent (a later lifecycle slice), left UNREPRESENTABLE
    48	 * (the `inline-declarative-packaged-only-in-l2-core` invariant's
    49	 * type/schema disposition). `requiresRuntimeContext` is read by
    50	 * admission (its cross-rule branch is P3).
    51	 */
    52	interface GateRegistrationBase {
    53	  readonly execution: "inline";
    54	  readonly requiresRuntimeContext: boolean;
    55	  validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw: unknown): GateConfigResult;
    56	}
    57	
    58	/**
    59	 * R2: the inline variant (`declarative` | `packaged`) additionally
    60	 * carries `evaluate`.
    61	 */
    62	export interface InlineGateRegistration extends GateRegistrationBase {
    63	  readonly implementation: "declarative" | "packaged";
    64	  evaluate(effectiveConfig: unknown, projection: GateProjection): GateDecision;
    65	}
    66	
    67	/**
    68	 * R2: a process-implementation registration carries NO `evaluate` — its
    69	 * execution path is `run_process_gate` (P3). The type-discriminated
    70	 * union makes the process arm unable to hold an evaluator.
    71	 */
    72	export interface ProcessGateRegistration extends GateRegistrationBase {
    73	  readonly implementation: "process";
    74	}
    75	
    76	export type GateRegistration = InlineGateRegistration | ProcessGateRegistration;
    77	
    78	/**
    79	 * R3: the injected catalog. `resolve` maps a binding's `uses` VALUE to
    80	 * its registration; `null` = unknown, which ADMISSION maps to the
    81	 * `gate_evaluator_unavailable` definition issue (A3). No mutation API
    82	 * (note 5): the registry is composition, not a lookup table.
    83	 */
    84	export interface GateCatalog {
    85	  resolve(uses: string): GateRegistration | null;
    86	}
    87	
    88	/**
    89	 * ch11-P3a R2: `ProcessResult` — the runner's per-invocation outcome
    90	 * (C34's verbatim field list at the TS grain). `exitCode` (an integer)
    91	 * and `stdout` (the UTF-8-decoded text) are present IFF kind=`"ok"` —
    92	 * expressed by the discriminated union (both iff directions are
    93	 * type-level). `logRef` is a nonempty string addressing the durably
    94	 * persisted evidence record; `durationMs` a non-negative integer. `kind`
    95	 * records PROCESS EXECUTION independent of decision classification (a
    96	 * malformed-JSON run is still kind=`"ok"`). `stdout` is process-returned
    97	 * text — classified untrusted-confined (never re-parsed or interpreted at
    98	 * this packet; C25's consumption is P3b's).
    99	 */
   100	export type ProcessResult =
   101	  | {
   102	      readonly kind: "ok";
   103	      readonly exitCode: number;
   104	      readonly stdout: string;
   105	      readonly logRef: string;
   106	      readonly durationMs: number;
   107	    }
   108	  | { readonly kind: "timeout"; readonly logRef: string; readonly durationMs: number }
   109	  | { readonly kind: "runner_error"; readonly logRef: string; readonly durationMs: number };
   110	
   111	/**
   112	 * ch11-P3a R1: the `ProcessGateRunner` port — the executor that spawns an
   113	 * external gate process. The kernel owns the CONTRACT; the runner owns the
   114	 * spawn (ch9's realization; this packet declares the port and scripts it in
   115	 * the kit). `command` is C13's one POSIX shell line; `cwd` is the workspace
   116	 * root; `stdin` carries the invocation document (no argv payload); `timeoutMs`
   117	 * is the C13/C16/C18 rename culture over the model's `timeout_ms`. `run()`
   118	 * resolves only after its evidence record is durably persisted (R3).
   119	 */
   120	export interface ProcessGateRunner {
   121	  run(
   122	    command: string,
   123	    options: { readonly cwd: string; readonly stdin: string; readonly timeoutMs: number },
   124	  ): Promise<ProcessResult>;
   125	}
   126	
   127	/**
   128	 * ch11-P3a R3: the evidence record (C26's complete field list, realized IN
   129	 * FULL — only the measurement is ch9's). Addressed by the `ProcessResult`'s
   130	 * `logRef` (the ref addresses the WHOLE record); the record itself carries no
   131	 * back-ref. `exitCode` is present IFF kind=`"ok"` (the discriminated union).
   132	 * `durationMs` a non-negative integer; `headSha`/`gitStatusHash` are the
   133	 * runner's DECLARED workspace facts (the kit mints deterministic fakes; ch9
   134	 * measures them). `log` is captured output text — untrusted-confined, retained
   135	 * verbatim, never re-parsed nor policy/path input. PERSISTENCE GUARANTEE:
   136	 * `run()` has durably persisted this record BEFORE returning; timeout and
   137	 * runner_error runs are evidenced equally.
   138	 */
   139	export type ProcessGateEvidence =
   140	  | {
   141	      readonly log: string;
   142	      readonly kind: "ok";
   143	      readonly exitCode: number;
   144	      readonly durationMs: number;
   145	      readonly headSha: string;
   146	      readonly gitStatusHash: string;
   147	    }
   148	  | {
   149	      readonly log: string;
   150	      readonly kind: "timeout";
   151	      readonly durationMs: number;
   152	      readonly headSha: string;
   153	      readonly gitStatusHash: string;
   154	    }
   155	  | {
   156	      readonly log: string;
   157	      readonly kind: "runner_error";
   158	      readonly durationMs: number;
   159	      readonly headSha: string;
   160	      readonly gitStatusHash: string;
   161	    };
   162	
   163	/**
   164	 * ch11-P3b C24: the wire projection — the compact snake_case rendering of the
   165	 * domain `GateProjection` the in-process evaluators read. A PURE key-rename
   166	 * projection over the SAME derived snapshot (no field added or dropped); each
   167	 * `history` entry's `step_id` is the step its transition was emitted FROM.
   168	 */
   169	export interface GateInvocationHistoryEntry {
   170	  readonly step_id: string;
   171	  readonly event_type: string;
   172	  readonly role: string;
   173	}
   174	export interface GateInvocationProjection {
   175	  readonly round: number;
   176	  readonly current_step: string;
   177	  readonly event_type: string;
   178	  readonly history: readonly GateInvocationHistoryEntry[];
   179	}
   180	
   181	/**
   182	 * ch11-P3b C23: the `GateInvocation` wire document — ONE UTF-8-encoded JSON
   183	 * value on the runner's stdin. The top-level keyset is snake_case
   184	 * model-verbatim `{instance_id, template_ref, step_id, event_type,
   185	 * expected_version, config, projection}`; `step_id` is the instance's current
   186	 * step, `event_type` the envelope's type, `expected_version` the instance's CAS
   187	 * version at evaluation. `config` is the admitted EFFECTIVE config VERBATIM —
   188	 * it rides with ITS OWN (camelCase) keys, deep-equal to the admitted binding's
   189	 * config (wire ≡ effective, the one-downstream-form rule; the envelope keyset
   190	 * snake_case, the config payload the effective form's — C23's letter).
   191	 * `projection` is the C24 compact form. The runner passes NOTHING else (no
   192	 * argv payload; C13's one-command-line + stdin contract).
   193	 */
   194	export interface GateInvocation {
   195	  readonly instance_id: string;
   196	  readonly template_ref: { readonly id: string; readonly version: number };
   197	  readonly step_id: string;
   198	  readonly event_type: string;
   199	  readonly expected_version: number;
   200	  readonly config: unknown;
   201	  readonly projection: GateInvocationProjection;
   202	}

v3/src/definition/load.ts:38:const EMPTY_CATALOG: GateCatalog = { resolve: () => null };
     1	import type { GateDecision, GateProjection } from "../domain/index.js";
     2	import type { GateConfigFinding, GateConfigResult, InlineGateRegistration } from "../ports/index.js";
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * `declarative.threshold` (packet ch11-P2a, G3/G4): the tiny declarative
     6	 * DSL over one allowlisted `gate_projection` path. Config is REQUIRED
     7	 * (C5); the exact own-key set is {metric, op, value}, all three
     8	 * required; `metric` ∈ {round}, `op` ∈ {">="}, `value` a safe integer
     9	 * ≥ 1 (the C12 VALUE half). Every violation is an UNCODED admission
    10	 * finding at the config's C7 path (C21 assigns this lane no code).
    11	 *
    12	 * Own-property discipline (G8): member reads and the unknown-key scan
    13	 * run over the value's OWN enumerable string keys only — a `__proto__`
    14	 * or inherited member is never read as config.
    15	 */
    16	
    17	const ALLOWED_KEYS = ["metric", "op", "value"];
    18	
    19	function isConfigMap(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    20	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    21	}
    22	
    23	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    24	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    25	}
    26	
    27	interface ThresholdConfig {
    28	  readonly metric: "round";
    29	  readonly op: ">=";
    30	  readonly value: number;
    31	}
    32	
    33	function validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw: unknown): GateConfigResult {
    34	  if (raw === undefined) {
    35	    return { ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "declarative.threshold requires a config" }] };
    36	  }
    37	  // Container precondition (C21 / note 3): a non-map config is ONE
    38	  // finding, dependent lanes suppressed — no per-key loop over it.
    39	  if (!isConfigMap(raw)) {
    40	    return { ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "config must be a map" }] };
    41	  }
    42	  const findings: GateConfigFinding[] = [];
    43	  for (const key of Object.keys(raw)) {
    44	    if (!ALLOWED_KEYS.includes(key)) {
    45	      findings.push({ path: key, message: `unknown config key '${key}' (allowed: metric, op, value)` });
    46	    }
    47	  }
    48	  const metric = ownGet(raw, "metric");
    49	  if (metric === undefined) {
    50	    findings.push({ path: "metric", message: "metric is required" });
    51	  } else if (metric !== "round") {
    52	    findings.push({ path: "metric", message: "metric must be 'round' (the only allowlisted projection path)" });
    53	  }
    54	  const op = ownGet(raw, "op");
    55	  if (op === undefined) {
    56	    findings.push({ path: "op", message: "op is required" });
    57	  } else if (op !== ">=") {
    58	    findings.push({ path: "op", message: "op must be '>='" });
    59	  }
    60	  const value = ownGet(raw, "value");
    61	  if (value === undefined) {
    62	    findings.push({ path: "value", message: "value is required" });
    63	  } else if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 1) {
    64	    // The numeric-identity rung is satisfied by the range check: `-0`,
    65	    // NaN, Infinity, non-integers, unsafe integers, and non-numbers all
    66	    // fail `Number.isSafeInteger(...) && value >= 1`.
    67	    findings.push({ path: "value", message: "value must be a safe integer >= 1" });
    68	  }
    69	  const [first, ...rest] = findings;
    70	  if (first !== undefined) {
    71	    // Nonempty by construction — the tuple type carries it (R4).
    72	    return { ok: false, findings: [first, ...rest] };
    73	  }
    74	  const effective: ThresholdConfig = { metric: "round", op: ">=", value: value as number };
    75	  return { ok: true, effective };
    76	}
    77	
    78	function evaluate(effectiveConfig: unknown, projection: GateProjection): GateDecision {
    79	  const { value } = effectiveConfig as ThresholdConfig;
    80	  if (projection.round < value) {
    81	    return { verdict: "block", reason: "sys:round_below_min" };
    82	  }
    83	  return { verdict: "allow" };
    84	}
    85	
    86	export const thresholdRegistration: InlineGateRegistration = {
    87	  implementation: "declarative",
    88	  execution: "inline",
    89	  requiresRuntimeContext: false,
    90	  validateAndNormalizeConfig,
    91	  evaluate,
    92	};
     1	import type { GateDecision, GateProjection } from "../domain/index.js";
     2	import type { GateConfigFinding, GateConfigResult, InlineGateRegistration } from "../ports/index.js";
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * `pairflow.previous_reviewer_verdict` (packet ch11-P2a, G5/G6): the
     6	 * packaged pure pairflow policy. Config is OPTIONAL (C5/C11): ABSENT ⇒
     7	 * effective `{required: true}` MATERIALIZED here (the behavior-
     8	 * preserving default, stable per C30). When present the exact own-key
     9	 * set is {required} and the sole legal value is `true` — `required:
    10	 * false` (the reserved future toggle, C11's fail-closed nature) and
    11	 * every other violation are UNCODED admission findings (C21).
    12	 *
    13	 * Own-property discipline (G8): the unknown-key scan and member reads
    14	 * run over the value's OWN enumerable string keys only.
    15	 */
    16	
    17	function isConfigMap(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    18	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    19	}
    20	
    21	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    22	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    23	}
    24	
    25	interface PreviousReviewerVerdictConfig {
    26	  readonly required: true;
    27	}
    28	
    29	const EFFECTIVE: PreviousReviewerVerdictConfig = { required: true };
    30	
    31	function validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw: unknown): GateConfigResult {
    32	  if (raw === undefined) {
    33	    return { ok: true, effective: EFFECTIVE };
    34	  }
    35	  // Container precondition (C21 / note 3): ONE finding, no key cascade.
    36	  if (!isConfigMap(raw)) {
    37	    return { ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "config must be a map" }] };
    38	  }
    39	  const findings: GateConfigFinding[] = [];
    40	  for (const key of Object.keys(raw)) {
    41	    if (key !== "required") {
    42	      findings.push({ path: key, message: `unknown config key '${key}' (allowed: required)` });
    43	    }
    44	  }
    45	  const required = ownGet(raw, "required");
    46	  if (required === undefined) {
    47	    findings.push({ path: "required", message: "required is required when a config is present" });
    48	  } else if (required !== true) {
    49	    findings.push({
    50	      path: "required",
    51	      message: "required must be true (false is a reserved future toggle)",
    52	    });
    53	  }
    54	  const [first, ...rest] = findings;
    55	  if (first !== undefined) {
    56	    // Nonempty by construction — the tuple type carries it (R4).
    57	    return { ok: false, findings: [first, ...rest] };
    58	  }
    59	  return { ok: true, effective: EFFECTIVE };
    60	}
    61	
    62	function evaluate(_effectiveConfig: unknown, projection: GateProjection): GateDecision {
    63	  const hasPriorFromThisStep = projection.history.some(
    64	    (entry) => entry.stepId === projection.currentStep,
    65	  );
    66	  if (hasPriorFromThisStep) {
    67	    return { verdict: "allow" };
    68	  }
    69	  return { verdict: "block", reason: "sys:no_previous_verdict" };
    70	}
    71	
    72	export const previousReviewerVerdictRegistration: InlineGateRegistration = {
    73	  implementation: "packaged",
    74	  execution: "inline",
    75	  requiresRuntimeContext: false,
    76	  validateAndNormalizeConfig,
    77	  evaluate,
    78	};
     1	import type { EffectiveProcessConfig } from "../domain/index.js";
     2	import type {
     3	  GateConfigFinding,
     4	  GateConfigResult,
     5	  ProcessGateRegistration,
     6	} from "../ports/index.js";
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * `external.process` (packet ch11-P3a, G2/V1–V4): the process-gate
    10	 * registration. It carries NO `evaluate` — a process gate runs via the
    11	 * `ProcessGateRunner` port (P3b), not an in-process evaluator. Its
    12	 * `validateAndNormalizeConfig` body IS the model's `validate_gate_config`
    13	 * unit: invoked by ADMISSION (`admitTemplate`) at definition load, never by
    14	 * CREATE and never mid-run. Failures are DEFINITION ISSUES; defaults
    15	 * MATERIALIZE here into the effective config (resolved once — downstream,
    16	 * including the P3b process wire, reads only the effective form).
    17	 *
    18	 * The BUILD TARGET is the canonical V-matrix, not the model skeleton: the
    19	 * o/p disposition lanes are DISTINCT single-code lanes (a `failInstance`
    20	 * value yields exactly one `gate_config_not_supported` finding, never the
    21	 * o+p double), and the `reason` grammar / exitCode-mode materialization /
    22	 * unknown-key + container-kind checks that the skeleton omits ARE realized.
    23	 *
    24	 * Own-property discipline (G8, `threshold.ts` pattern): every config read and
    25	 * every unknown-key scan runs over OWN enumerable string keys only — a
    26	 * `__proto__` or inherited member is never read as config.
    27	 */
    28	
    29	/** The definition-issue codes this registration assigns (V4 / the operative
    30	 * material's data line). The tokens reuse the former rejection names but ride
    31	 * ch8's load channel as ISSUE codes. */
    32	const CODE_INVALID = "invalid_process_gate_config";
    33	const CODE_NOT_SUPPORTED = "gate_config_not_supported";
    34	
    35	/** The `reason` token grammar (V2 lane q). */
    36	const TOKEN_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/;
    37	
    38	/** The C17 exitCode-mode default reason tokens, per bucket. */
    39	const DEFAULT_REASON_ZERO = "sys:exit_zero";
    40	const DEFAULT_REASON_NONZERO = "sys:exit_nonzero";
    41	
    42	const TOP_KEYS = ["command", "timeoutMs", "output", "onExit", "onRunnerError", "onTimeout", "reason"];
    43	const OUTPUT_MODES = ["exitCode", "gateDecisionJson"] as const;
    44	const VERDICTS = ["allow", "warn", "block"] as const;
    45	const DISPOSITION_KEYS = ["onRunnerError", "onTimeout"] as const;
    46	const BUCKETS = ["zero", "nonzero"] as const;
    47	
    48	type OutputMode = (typeof OUTPUT_MODES)[number];
    49	type Verdict = (typeof VERDICTS)[number];
    50	
    51	// `EffectiveProcessConfig` HOME is `domain/gate.ts` (ch11-P3b): the kernel
    52	// branch and this validator share ONE shape. This module remains its sole
    53	// producer (materialize-once at admission); the kernel is a typed reader.
    54	
    55	function isConfigMap(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
    56	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
    57	}
    58	
    59	function ownGet(record: Record<string, unknown>, key: string): unknown {
    60	  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(record, key) ? record[key] : undefined;
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** Read an own string token from a validated `reason` map (success path). */
    64	function reasonToken(reason: Record<string, unknown>, bucket: string): string | undefined {
    65	  const value = ownGet(reason, bucket);
    66	  return typeof value === "string" ? value : undefined;
    67	}
    68	
    69	function validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw: unknown): GateConfigResult {
    70	  // Lane a: config is REQUIRED (C5) — uncoded, at the config path.
    71	  if (raw === undefined) {
    72	    return { ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "external.process requires a config" }] };
    73	  }
    74	  // Lane b: container precondition — a non-map config is ONE finding, its
    75	  // dependent lanes suppressed (no per-key cascade). Uncoded.
    76	  if (!isConfigMap(raw)) {
    77	    return { ok: false, findings: [{ path: "", message: "config must be a map" }] };
    78	  }
    79	
    80	  const findings: GateConfigFinding[] = [];
    81	
    82	  // Lane c: unknown top-level process-config key — uncoded.
    83	  for (const key of Object.keys(raw)) {
    84	    if (!TOP_KEYS.includes(key)) {
    85	      findings.push({ path: key, message: `unknown config key '${key}' (allowed: ${TOP_KEYS.join(", ")})` });
    86	    }
    87	  }
    88	
    89	  // Lane d: `command` missing / empty / non-string → coded.
    90	  const command = ownGet(raw, "command");
    91	  if (command === undefined) {
    92	    findings.push({ path: "command", code: CODE_INVALID, message: "command is required" });
    93	  } else if (typeof command !== "string" || command === "") {
    94	    findings.push({ path: "command", code: CODE_INVALID, message: "command must be a non-empty string" });
    95	  }
    96	
    97	  // Lane e: `timeoutMs` missing or value-invalid per V3 (safe integer ≥ 1;
    98	  // the full numeric ladder — `-0`, NaN, Infinity, non-integer, unsafe,
    99	  // boxed, non-number all fail `Number.isSafeInteger(...) && >= 1`) → coded.
   100	  const timeoutMs = ownGet(raw, "timeoutMs");
   101	  if (timeoutMs === undefined) {
   102	    findings.push({ path: "timeoutMs", code: CODE_INVALID, message: "timeoutMs is required" });
   103	  } else if (typeof timeoutMs !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs < 1) {
   104	    findings.push({ path: "timeoutMs", code: CODE_INVALID, message: "timeoutMs must be a safe integer >= 1" });
   105	  }
   106	
   107	  // `output` — the mode is `exitCode` by default (C14); an unknown-value mode
   108	  // is the only invalid form. `mode` stays `undefined` (unresolved) when the
   109	  // container or value is bad, suppressing the mode-conditional onExit lanes.
   110	  const output = ownGet(raw, "output");
   111	  let mode: OutputMode | undefined;
   112	  if (output === undefined) {
   113	    mode = "exitCode";
   114	  } else if (!isConfigMap(output)) {
   115	    // Lane f: container precondition — suppress g/h and the onExit lanes.
   116	    findings.push({ path: "output", message: "output must be a map" });
   117	  } else {
   118	    // Lane g: unknown inner key — uncoded.
   119	    for (const key of Object.keys(output)) {
   120	      if (key !== "mode") {
   121	        findings.push({ path: `output.${key}`, message: `unknown output key '${key}' (allowed: mode)` });
   122	      }
   123	    }
   124	    const rawMode = ownGet(output, "mode");
   125	    if (rawMode === undefined) {
   126	      mode = "exitCode"; // the defaulted form
   127	    } else if (rawMode === "exitCode" || rawMode === "gateDecisionJson") {
   128	      mode = rawMode;
   129	    } else {
   130	      // Lane h: `output.mode` not in the allowlist (non-string included) → coded.
   131	      findings.push({
   132	        path: "output.mode",
   133	        code: CODE_INVALID,
   134	        message: `output.mode must be one of ${OUTPUT_MODES.join(", ")}`,
   135	      });
   136	    }
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  // `onExit` — mode-conditional. In exitCode mode it is REQUIRED and its
   140	  // buckets are validated (lanes i/j/k/l/m). In gateDecisionJson mode its
   141	  // mere presence is UNCONSUMED config (lane n, uncoded). When `mode` is
   142	  // unresolved the lanes are suppressed (the container/value fault already
   143	  // fired).
   144	  const onExit = ownGet(raw, "onExit");
   145	  if (mode === "exitCode") {
   146	    if (onExit === undefined) {
   147	      // Lane i: missing in exitCode mode (the defaulted form included) → coded.
   148	      findings.push({ path: "onExit", code: CODE_INVALID, message: "onExit is required in exitCode mode" });
   149	    } else if (!isConfigMap(onExit)) {
   150	      // Lane j: container precondition — suppress k/l/m.
   151	      findings.push({ path: "onExit", message: "onExit must be a map" });
   152	    } else {
   153	      // Lane m: surplus key — uncoded.
   154	      for (const key of Object.keys(onExit)) {
   155	        if (key !== "zero" && key !== "nonzero") {
   156	          findings.push({ path: `onExit.${key}`, message: `unknown onExit key '${key}' (allowed: zero, nonzero)` });
   157	        }
   158	      }
   159	      for (const bucket of BUCKETS) {
   160	        const value = ownGet(onExit, bucket);
   161	        if (value === undefined) {
   162	          // Lane k: bucket missing → coded.
   163	          findings.push({ path: `onExit.${bucket}`, code: CODE_INVALID, message: `onExit.${bucket} is required` });
   164	        } else if (value !== "allow" && value !== "warn" && value !== "block") {
   165	          // Lane l: bucket value outside the verdict allowlist (`route` included) → coded.
   166	          findings.push({
   167	            path: `onExit.${bucket}`,
   168	            code: CODE_INVALID,
   169	            message: `onExit.${bucket} must be one of ${VERDICTS.join(", ")}`,
   170	          });
   171	        }
   172	      }
   173	    }
   174	  } else if (mode === "gateDecisionJson" && onExit !== undefined) {
   175	    // Lane n: onExit present in gateDecisionJson mode (unconsumed config) — uncoded.
   176	    findings.push({ path: "onExit", message: "onExit is not consumed in gateDecisionJson mode" });
   177	  }
   178	
   179	  // Dispositions (lanes o/p). DISTINCT single-code lanes: `failInstance` is
   180	  // the reserved-disposition lane (o, `gate_config_not_supported`); any OTHER
   181	  // non-`blockTransition` value is lane p (`invalid_process_gate_config`).
   182	  // The if/else-if forecloses the o+p double the skeleton's fall-through
   183	  // would produce (the operative-material authority note).
   184	  for (const key of DISPOSITION_KEYS) {
   185	    const disposition = ownGet(raw, key);
   186	    if (disposition === undefined) {
   187	      continue; // absent ⇒ blockTransition materializes into the effective config
   188	    }
   189	    if (disposition === "failInstance") {
   190	      findings.push({
   191	        path: key,
   192	        code: CODE_NOT_SUPPORTED,
   193	        message: `${key}: failInstance is a reserved future disposition`,
   194	      });
   195	    } else if (disposition !== "blockTransition") {
   196	      findings.push({ path: key, code: CODE_INVALID, message: `${key} must be blockTransition` });
   197	    }
   198	  }
   199	
   200	  // Lane q: `reason` — a non-map value is a CONTAINER PRECONDITION (one
   201	  // finding, sub-lanes suppressed, symmetric with b/f/j); on a map, an
   202	  // unknown key or a token failing the grammar is uncoded.
   203	  const reason = ownGet(raw, "reason");
   204	  if (reason !== undefined) {
   205	    if (!isConfigMap(reason)) {
   206	      findings.push({ path: "reason", message: "reason must be a map" });
   207	    } else {
   208	      for (const key of Object.keys(reason)) {
   209	        if (key !== "zero" && key !== "nonzero") {
   210	          findings.push({ path: `reason.${key}`, message: `unknown reason key '${key}' (allowed: zero, nonzero)` });
   211	          continue;
   212	        }
   213	        const token = ownGet(reason, key);
   214	        if (typeof token !== "string" || !TOKEN_RE.test(token)) {
   215	          findings.push({ path: `reason.${key}`, message: `reason.${key} must match ${TOKEN_RE.source}` });
   216	        }
   217	      }
   218	    }
   219	  }
   220	
   221	  const [first, ...rest] = findings;
   222	  if (first !== undefined) {
   223	    // Nonempty by construction — the tuple type carries it (R4).
   224	    return { ok: false, findings: [first, ...rest] };
   225	  }
   226	
   227	  // Success: materialize the effective config ONCE. Every read below is
   228	  // known-valid (a finding would have returned above); `mode` is resolved.
   229	  const effectiveMode: OutputMode = mode ?? "exitCode";
   230	  const base = {
   231	    command: command as string,
   232	    timeoutMs: timeoutMs as number,
   233	    output: { mode: effectiveMode },
   234	    onRunnerError: "blockTransition" as const,
   235	    onTimeout: "blockTransition" as const,
   236	  };
   237	  const reasonMap = isConfigMap(reason) ? reason : undefined;
   238	
   239	  if (effectiveMode === "exitCode") {
   240	    const onExitMap = onExit as Record<string, unknown>;

exec
