### F8 — A három gate-validator kizárásának indoka pontos, és a migráció valódi boundary act

CLASS: confirmed-sound  
LENS:  2c  
WHAT: A jelenlegi modulhatár ténylegesen megakadályozza, hogy a `gates/**` érték-importálja a definition engine-t, a befecskendezett `GateRegistration` pedig csak egy opaque `validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw)` callbacket ad át, deklarációt és csatorna-/source-contextet nem; ezért a migráció nem lehet helyi validator-csere.  
WHERE: `v3/eslint.config.mjs:476-508`; `v3/src/ports/gate.ts:31-56`, `:76-86`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:988-1018`; `v3/src/gates/{threshold,previousReviewerVerdict,process}.ts`  
EVIDENCE: A lint allowlist szerint production `src/gates/**` csak relatív siblinget, `../domain/`-t és `../ports/`-ot value-importálhat; `../definition/schema/engine` tiltott. A port egyetlen config-felülete `validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw: unknown)`, és az engine delegate-je ezt hívja; nincs declaration payload, channel, YAML node vagy source slice. Így a három schema engine-re hozása a regisztráció port alakját és a definition↔gates felelősséghatárt is érintené, a ch11-C12 source-ladderhez pedig a csatornaoperandus átadását is.  
WHY IT MATTERS: A buildben rögzített halasztás nem kifogás és nem rejtett egyszerű munka: a későbbi migráció pontosan az ADR D6 szerinti külön ratifikált surface/boundary döntést igényli, különösen mert a validatorok normalizált effective configot is termelnek.

v3/implementation/contracts/ch11-gate-format-contract.md:219:| C38 | ABSENT-KEY DEFAULT (DECIDED HERE — the model exhibits the key but leaves the absent case unstated; RE-DECIDED at the ratifier read 2026-07-12, superseding the panel rounds' start-arrival default): an absent `round` key declares NO advancing transitions — the C39 expansion runs on the EMPTY set, every per-transition flag is false, and after ACTIVATION the instance's round remains 1 for the rest of active execution (the CREATE-state round is 0 and activation sets 1 — the model's fixed lifecycle, untouched by this row). Rationale: the LEAST-INFERENCE reading of declared transition semantics — an undeclared advancement is inferred from NOTHING, not even as a default (a start-arrival default would re-admit the banned graph-shape inference at the authoring layer); the ch-4 `target === template.start` heuristic was PROVISIONAL kernel scaffolding (`instance.ts`'s own forward pointer: "Formal round machinery ... is L2"), never a ratified format default — no RELEASED v3 template-compatibility baseline exists to preserve (the repo's canonical `v3/templates/local-pair-v0@1.yaml` is an in-repo development artifact; its gaining the round declaration is an explicit P4 migration), and §8.2 rule 5 binds from THIS ratification on, to THIS default. The model's exhibited start-arrival behavior is RESTORED per template by EXPLICIT declaration (the shipped template file gains its `round:` declaration at P4; the l2 trace's direct-constructed template declares it at P2c). CONSEQUENCE (stated, deliberate): a template binding a round-reading gate (C10) with NO round declaration is LEGAL-but-INERT — round never exceeds 1, a `value ≥ 2` threshold never passes; authoring-side detection of that shape (an admission cross-rule or a lint warning) is a NAMED later additive decision, deliberately NOT taken here. The (empty) default is MATERIALIZED once at admission (the C14/C17 culture; the admitted form carries no absent state), and an AUTHORED list is EXHAUSTIVE: exactly the listed steps advance — nothing is implicit, the start step included (replace, not merge). RELATION to plan §8.2 rule 2 + ch8-C7 (stated, not inferred): both expect an additive key's default to be BEHAVIOR-PRESERVING, and this default DEVIATES — a declaration-absent file does NOT keep the pre-key (ch-4 provisional heuristic) round behavior. The deviation is legal under §8.2 rule 2's DEVIATION CLAUSE — a non-behavior-preserving default ONLY as the ratifier's explicit per-key ratification act, recorded in the realizing chapter's contract row — and THIS row is that clause's FIRST exercise (the clause and this exercise are ratified by the same re-ratification act), scoped to the `round` key alone: every other key's behavior-preserving obligation stands. ch8-C7's realized text is untouched — its own post-realized carrier clause ENUMERATES the successor surface as a permissible carrier ("a new ratification act on this draft vs a successor surface — ... the process layer's call at that chapter"), and the selection is exactly the process-layer call this reopen exercises. |

   310	const PENDING = { status: "pending" } as const;
   311	
   312	/** Walk a declaration path over the value graph, expanding `*` over open
   313	 * maps. `..` resolves one level up from the citing frame. A prefix that
   314	 * has not been evaluated yet answers PENDING, not "unreliable". */
   315	function resolvePath(
   316	  run: Run,
   317	  from: Frame,
   318	  path: DeclPath,
   319	): { readonly status: "ok"; readonly nodes: unknown[] } | { readonly status: "unreliable" | "pending" } {
   320	  const segments = path.split(".");
   321	  let nodes: unknown[];
   322	  let declPrefix: string;
   323	  const head = segments.shift();
   324	  if (head === "$") {
   325	    nodes = [run.root];
   326	    declPrefix = "$";
   327	  } else if (head === "^") {
   328	    // Parent-relative: the citing node's own container.
   329	    nodes = [from.parentValue];
   330	    declPrefix = from.decl.split(".").slice(0, -1).join(".");
   331	  } else {
   332	    return UNRELIABLE;
   333	  }
   334	  for (const segment of segments) {
   335	    declPrefix = `${declPrefix}.${segment}`;
   336	    if (!run.reliable(declPrefix)) return UNRELIABLE;
   337	    if (!run.completed.has(declPrefix)) return PENDING;
   338	    const next: unknown[] = [];
   339	    for (const node of nodes) {
   340	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   341	      if (segment === "*") {
   342	        for (const [key, child] of entriesOf(node)) {
   343	          if (typeof key === "string") next.push(child);
   344	        }
   345	      } else {
   346	        if (!containerHas(node, segment)) return UNRELIABLE;
   347	        next.push(containerGet(node, segment));
   348	      }
   349	    }
   350	    nodes = next;
   351	  }
   352	  return { status: "ok", nodes };
   353	}
   354	
   355	function evaluateSelector(run: Run, from: Frame, selector: Selector): SelectorResult {
   356	  if ("injected" in selector) {
   357	    // D8's ADMITTED widening: the selector root is an INJECTED set. A
   358	    // registry resolves rather than enumerates, so membership is asked of
   359	    // it directly (`isMember`); the set itself is never listed.
   360	    return run.registry(selector.injected) === undefined ? UNRELIABLE : { status: "ok", values: [] };
   361	  }
   362	  if ("union" in selector) {
   363	    const parts = selector.union.map((part) => evaluateSelector(run, from, part));
   364	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "pending")) return PENDING;
   365	    if (parts.some((part) => part.status === "unreliable")) return UNRELIABLE;
   366	    return {
   367	      status: "ok",
   368	      values: parts.flatMap((part) => (part.status === "ok" ? part.values : [])),
   369	    };
   370	  }
   371	  const target = "keysOf" in selector ? selector.keysOf : "valuesOf" in selector ? selector.valuesOf : selector.collect;
   372	  const resolved = resolvePath(run, from, target);
   373	  if (resolved.status !== "ok") return resolved;
   374	  const values: string[] = [];
   375	  for (const node of resolved.nodes) {
   376	    if ("keysOf" in selector) {
   377	      if (!isContainer(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   378	      for (const [key] of entriesOf(node)) if (typeof key === "string") values.push(key);
   379	    } else if ("valuesOf" in selector) {
   380	      if (!Array.isArray(node)) return UNRELIABLE;
   381	      for (const item of node) if (typeof item === "string") values.push(item);
   382	    } else {
   383	      if (typeof node !== "string") return UNRELIABLE;
   384	      values.push(node);
   385	    }
   386	  }
   387	  return { status: "ok", values };
   388	}
   389	
   390	/** `memberOf` against an INJECTED root asks the registry; against a
   391	 * document root it asks the collected set. */
   392	function isMember(run: Run, selector: Selector, set: SelectorResult, candidate: string): boolean {
   393	  if ("injected" in selector) return run.registry(selector.injected)?.resolve(candidate) != null;
   394	  return set.status === "ok" && set.values.includes(candidate);
   395	}
   396	
   397	function checkMembership(
   398	  run: Run,
   399	  frame: Frame,
   400	  rule: MembershipRule,
   401	  candidate: unknown,
   402	  slots: Slots,
   403	  local?: ReadonlyMap<string, boolean>,
   404	): void {
   405	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false || !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;
   406	  const set = evaluateSelector(run, frame, rule.target);
   407	  if (set.status === "pending") {
   408	    run.deferred.push({ frame, rule, candidate, slots });
   409	    return;
   410	  }
   411	  if (set.status === "unreliable") return;
   412	  const at = rule.at === "container" ? parentPath(frame.path) : frame.path;
   413	  const hit = typeof candidate === "string" && isMember(run, rule.target, set, candidate);
   414	  const violated = rule.relation === "disjointFrom" ? hit : !hit;
   415	  if (violated) {
   416	    run.emit(at, render(rule.message, { ...slots, path: at, value: candidate }), rule.code);
   417	  }
   418	}

### F10 — A dokumentált parent-relative path szintaxis érvénytelen az interpreterben

CLASS: design-error  
LENS:  4b  
WHAT: A vocabulary `DeclPath` dokumentációja vezető `..`-ot ír elő a parent-relative címzéshez, de az engine kizárólag `^` headet fogad el; a dokumentációt követő deklaráció lefordul, majd a selector lane csendben suppresselődik.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:43-48`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:312-352`; `v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:200-203`  
EVIDENCE: A live deklaráció maga `{ keysOf: "^.transitions" }` alakot használ. Az engine `path.split(".")` után csak `head === "$"` vagy `head === "^"` esetet kezel, különben `UNRELIABLE`-t ad; a membership ellenőrzés az unreliable targetnél finding nélkül visszatér. Konkrétan egy declaration-author a vocabulary 46. sorát követve `target: {keysOf: "..transitions"}`-t ír: a `DeclPath = string` típus elfogadja, az engine headje üres string lesz, és a dead-gate-config rule egyetlen inputon sem tüzel.  
WHY IT MATTERS: Ez a kért „reasonable declaration means nothing” eset: a language spec szerinti szerkesztés compile- és lint-green lehet, miközben egy teljes validációs obligation észrevétlenül megszűnik.

  1028	function evaluateEquals(run: Run, frame: Frame, rule: EqualsRuleDecl): void {
  1029	  if (rule.dependsOn?.some((tag) => !run.reliable(tag)) === true) return;

  1047	function runDeferred(run: Run): void {
  1048	  // A membership lane deferred for a PENDING operand runs once, in the
  1049	  // order the walk queued it. The queue is drained, never re-fed: every
  1050	  // operand has completed by now, so a second pass could only loop.
  1051	  const queued = run.deferred.splice(0, run.deferred.length);
  1052	  for (const check of queued) {
  1053	    checkMembership(run, check.frame, check.rule, check.candidate, check.slots);
  1054	  }
  1055	  run.deferred.length = 0;
  1056	}

### F11 — A `DeclPath` név alatt három eltérő nyelv fut

CLASS: design-error  
LENS:  4a  
WHAT: Ugyanaz a `DeclPath = string` típus egyszer value-graph selector pathot, egyszer finding-output path template-et, egyszer pedig normalizer-operándust jelent, eltérő gyökér-, wildcard- és placeholder-szabályokkal.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:43-59`, `:301-348`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:315-352`, `:1028-1044`; `v3/src/definition/schema/normalizer.ts:36-45`, `:88-103`  
EVIDENCE: A selector interpreter csak `$`/`^` gyökeret és `*` szegmenst ért. Az equals `missingFrom*.at` ugyanilyen `DeclPath`, de a live értékei `roles` és `roles.{valueRaw}`; az engine ezeket nem bejárja, hanem message-template-ként rendereli. A normalizer `at()` csak ponttal bontott `$`-utakat kezel, míg a második hook külön `split(".*.")` grammatikát használ. Egy packet-author ezért a type/docs alapján nem tudja megmondani, hogy például a `*` vagy `{valueRaw}` melyik `DeclPath` pozícióban legális vagy néma no-op.  
WHY IT MATTERS: A vocabulary nem koherens nyelvspecifikációként működik: az azonos típusnév kompatibilitást ígér olyan operandusok között, amelyek interpreterei inkompatibilisek, így a későbbi deklarációs növekedés compile-time védelem nélkül tud csendes szabályvesztést okozni.

   988	function evalDelegate(
   989	  run: Run,
   990	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "delegate" }>,
   991	  frame: Frame,
   992	  local?: LocalOk,
   993	): NodeResult {
   994	  if (decl.dependsOn?.some((tag) => local?.get(tag) === false) === true) return { ok: true };
   995	  const owner = frame.parentValue;
   996	  const by = isContainer(owner) ? containerGet(owner, decl.by) : undefined;
   997	  if (typeof by !== "string") return { ok: true };
   998	  const registration = run.registry(decl.registry)?.resolve(by) ?? null;
   999	  if (registration === null) return { ok: true };
  1000	  if (registration.requiresRuntimeContext) run.runtimeContextBindings.push(parentPath(frame.path));
  1001	  const raw = frame.value === undefined ? undefined : materialize(frame.value, run.memo);
  1002	  const result = registration.validateAndNormalizeConfig(raw);
  1003	  if (result.ok) {
  1004	    run.effectiveConfigs.set(parentPath(frame.path), result.effective);
  1005	    return { ok: true, value: result.effective };
  1006	  }
  1007	  if (result.findings.length === 0) {
  1008	    run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.beltMessage, { ...baseSlots(frame), key: by, value: by }));
  1009	    return { ok: false };
  1010	  }
  1011	  for (const finding of result.findings) {
  1012	    const at = finding.path === "" ? frame.path : `${frame.path}.${finding.path}`;
  1013	    run.emit(at, finding.message, finding.code);
  1014	  }
  1015	  return { ok: false };

### F12 — Az egyliterálos mezők érve a jelenlegi TS-authoring határán helyes

CLASS: confirmed-sound  
LENS:  4c  
WHAT: Ha a declaration kizárólag `SurfaceDecl`-ként typecheckelt TypeScript literalból jön, egy egyelemű literal mezőnél nincs más legális runtime ág, ezért önmagában nem defect, hogy az engine nem minden ilyen mezőt hasonlít külön; ez a garancia nem terjed ki mutációra, castolt vagy deszerializált adatra.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:272-286`, `:301-348`; `v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:479-545`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:988-1005`

WHY IT MATTERS: A build érve nem általános runtime-validációs állítás, hanem a jelenlegi authoring boundary garanciája. Ezen a határon sound; JSON-ból betöltött, plugin által adott vagy mutálható deklarációra változatlanul hivatkozni már hamis lenne.

   861	function evalString(
   862	  run: Run,
   863	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "string" }>,
   864	  frame: Frame,
   865	  local?: LocalOk,
   866	): NodeResult {
   867	  const value = frame.value;
   868	  const slots: Slots = { ...baseSlots(frame), grammar: decl.grammar?.re };
   869	  if (typeof value !== "string") {
   870	    // A node that declares NO type lane leaves the non-string case to its
   871	    // membership lane, which emits ONE finding for both faults (the
   872	    // measured form of `start` and of a transition target).
   873	    if (decl.typeMessage !== undefined) {
   874	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.typeMessage, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   875	      return { ok: false };
   876	    }
   877	  } else {
   878	    if (decl.nonempty !== undefined && value.length === 0) {
   879	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.nonempty.message, slots), decl.presence?.code);
   880	      return { ok: false };
   881	    }
   882	    if (decl.grammar !== undefined && !new RegExp(decl.grammar.re, "u").test(value)) {
   883	      run.emit(frame.path, render(decl.grammar.message, slots));
   884	      return { ok: false };
   885	    }
   886	  }
   887	  if (decl.memberOf !== undefined) {
   888	    const before = run.findings.length;
   889	    checkMembership(run, frame, decl.memberOf, value, slots, local);
   890	    if (run.findings.length > before) return { ok: false, value };
   891	  }
   892	  return typeof value === "string" ? { ok: true, value } : { ok: false };
   893	}
   894	
   895	function evalInteger(
   896	  run: Run,
   897	  decl: Extract<NodeDecl, { kind: "integer" }>,
   898	  frame: Frame,
   899	): NodeResult {
   900	  const value = frame.value;

47:const PART = {
48:  ref: "ref:\n  id: t\n  version: 1\n",
49:  start: "start: s\n",
50:  steps: "steps:\n  s:\n    role: r\n    instruction: i\n    transitions: {}\n",
51:  terminal: "terminal:\n  - done\n",

53:} as const;
54:type PartName = keyof typeof PART;
56:function template(overrides: Partial<Record<PartName, string>> = {}, drop: PartName[] = []): string {
57:  return (Object.keys(PART) as PartName[])
58:    .filter((name) => !drop.includes(name))
59:    .map((name) => overrides[name] ?? PART[name])
60:    .join("");

547:describe("V15 — acyclicity (cycle-safe validator)", () => {
548:  it("reports a cyclic value graph (through agentConfig) as a validate finding — no hang, no throw", () => {
549:    const err = expectValidateErr(
550:      template({
551:        steps: "steps:\n  s:\n    role: r\n    instruction: i\n    transitions: {}\n    agentConfig: &a\n      self: *a\n",
552:      }),
553:    );
554:    expect(err.findings.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
555:    expect(JSON.stringify(err.findings)).toMatch(/cycl/i);
556:  });
558:  // Aftermath round 2 (the arm's re-check catch): with accumulation the
559:  // walk RUNS on cyclic graphs — every message site must be cycle-safe.
560:  // A cyclic map planted in each arbitrary-value scalar slot: the cycle
561:  // finding survives, no internal-failure, no throw.
562:  const cyclicSlots = [
563:    ["ref.id (V2 message site)", "ref:\n  id: &a\n    self: *a\n  version: 1\n", "ref"],
564:    ["step role (grammar site)", "steps:\n  s:\n    role: &a\n      self: *a\n    instruction: i\n    transitions: {}\n", "steps"],
565:    ["start (V13 message site)", "start: &a\n  self: *a\n", "start"],
566:    ["transition target (V14 message site)", "steps:\n  s:\n    role: r\n    instruction: i\n    transitions:\n      GO: &a\n        self: *a\n", "steps"],
567:  ] as const;
568:  for (const [label, part, partName] of cyclicSlots) {
569:    it(`stays cycle-safe with a cyclic value at: ${label}`, () => {
570:      const err = expectValidateErr(template({ [partName]: part }));
571:      expect(JSON.stringify(err.findings)).toMatch(/cycl/i);
572:      expect(JSON.stringify(err.findings)).not.toMatch(/internal validator failure/);
573:    });
574:  }
576:  it("ACCUMULATES the cycle finding with the other structural lanes (aftermath — E2 has no cycle exemption)", () => {
577:    const err = expectValidateErr(
578:      template({
579:        start: "start: nope\n",
580:        steps: "steps:\n  s:\n    role: r\n    instruction: i\n    transitions: {}\n    agentConfig: &a\n      self: *a\n",
581:      }) + "extra: 1\n",
582:    );
583:    const p = paths(err);
584:    expect(JSON.stringify(err.findings)).toMatch(/cycl/i);
585:    expect(p).toContain("start");
586:    expect(p).toContain("extra");
587:    // ch12-p2 (C7): the cyclic value sits in agentConfig, so the admit
588:    // rung's canonical-JSON lane ALSO fires on it (a cycle is not
589:    // canonical-JSON-safe) and ACCUMULATES beside the walk's cycle/start/
590:    // extra findings — four, not three.
591:    expect(p).toContain("steps.s.agentConfig");
592:    expect(err.findings.length).toBe(4);
593:  });

758:  advanceOnArrivalAt:
759:    - implement

762:      ref: { id: "local-pair-v0", version: 1 },
763:      start: "implement",
764:      steps: {
765:        implement: {
766:          role: "implementer",
767:          instruction: "build it",
768:          transitions: { PASS: "review" },
769:          // ch11-P4 (Y4): the authored `round` declaration (F5 source-form
770:          // clean) expands to per-transition flags — a transition advances
771:          // iff its TARGET ∈ advanceOnArrivalAt. implement.PASS → review
772:          // (not listed) ⇒ false.
773:          advancesRound: { PASS: false },
774:        },
775:        review: {
776:          role: "reviewer",
777:          instruction: "review it",
778:          transitions: { PASS: "implement", CONVERGED: "done" },
779:          // review.PASS → implement (LISTED) ⇒ true; CONVERGED → done ⇒ false.
780:          advancesRound: { PASS: true, CONVERGED: false },
781:        },
782:      },
783:      terminal: ["done"],
784:      roles: {
785:        implementer: { defaultActor: "codex" },
786:        reviewer: { defaultActor: "claude" },
787:      },
788:      round: { advanceOnArrivalAt: ["implement"] },
789:      // ch12-p1b G3: admission materializes the activation default.
790:      activation: { mode: "immediate" },
791:      // ch12-p3 R1: admission materializes the runtime-context requirement
792:      // (absent ⇒ "none").
793:      runtimeContext: "none",
794:    };
795:    const result = load(canonical);
796:    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
797:    if (!result.ok) return;
798:    expect(result.template).toStrictEqual(expected);
799:  });

1330:  it("an unknown spec-map key → a finding at runtimeContext.<key>", () => {
1331:    const err = expectValidateErr(
1332:      withRc("  kind: worktree\n  provider: pairflow.worktree\n  extra: 1\n"),
1333:    );
1334:    expect(paths(err)).toContain("runtimeContext.extra");
1335:  });
1337:  it("RP6: `!!str none` is the string none (context-free) → admits", () => {
1338:    expect(load(`${VALID}runtimeContext: !!str none\n`).ok).toBe(true);
1339:  });
1341:  it("RP6: the capitalized-null family (`None`/`NULL`/`Null`) is a null node → admission's illegal-value container finding", () => {
1342:    for (const nul of ["None", "NULL", "Null"]) {
1343:      const err = expectValidateErr(`${VALID}runtimeContext: ${nul}\n`);
1344:      expect(paths(err)).toContain("runtimeContext");
1345:    }
1346:  });
1348:  it("RP6: a duplicate `kind` key is the document-wide DUPLICATE_KEY (a parse-stage refusal, never a walk finding)", () => {
1349:    const result = load(
1350:      withRc("  kind: worktree\n  kind: other\n  provider: pairflow.worktree\n"),
1351:    );
1352:    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
1353:    if (result.ok) return;
1354:    expect(result.error.stage).toBe("parse");
1355:  });
1357:  it("RP6: a merge key `<<` inside the spec map is a LITERAL key under 1.2 core → double fail-closed (unknown key + still-missing kind)", () => {
1358:    const err = expectValidateErr(
1359:      withRc("  <<:\n    kind: worktree\n  provider: pairflow.worktree\n"),
1360:    );
1361:    // `<<` is not a legal spec-map key (no 1.2 merge) → unknown key; and the
1362:    // map still lacks a legal `kind` → missing-key finding. A silent merge
1363:    // would leave a well-formed spec and this test would fail.
1364:    expect(paths(err)).toContain("runtimeContext.<<");
1365:    expect(paths(err)).toContain("runtimeContext");
1366:  });
1368:  it("RP6: a WHOLE-MAP alias spec map resolves to a plain object graph (no bypass) → the ENTIRE runtimeContext value materializes to a plain own-property record", () => {
1369:    // The WHOLE spec map is anchored on a step's format-open `agentConfig`
1370:    // (`&s`) and the ENTIRE `runtimeContext` value is that alias (`*s`) — this
1371:    // exercises WHOLE-MAP alias resolution/materialization (the value is a
1372:    // `mapAsMap` alias node, not an ordinary map). A raw JS Map or an
1373:    // unresolved alias node slipping through the whole-value alias path fails
1374:    // toEqual (a Map is not deep-equal to a plain object).
1375:    const wholeMapAlias = `ref:
1376:  id: t
1377:  version: 1
1378:start: s
1380:  s:
1381:    role: r
1382:    instruction: i
1383:    transitions: {}

1386:      provider: pairflow.worktree
1387:      config:

1390:  - done
1392:  r: {}
1393:runtimeContext: *s
1395:    const result = load(wholeMapAlias);
1396:    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
1397:    if (!result.ok) return;
1398:    expect(result.template.runtimeContext).toEqual({
1399:      kind: "worktree",
1400:      provider: "pairflow.worktree",

1402:    });
1403:  });
1405:  it("RP6: an INNER anchor/alias in the spec map resolves to a plain value graph (no alias node leaks) — the inner-alias dimension", () => {
1406:    // The provider string is anchored and aliased inside `config.mirror`; the
1407:    // whole template is valid and admits. Complements the whole-map alias test

import { isAlias, isScalar } from "yaml";
import type { Document } from "yaml";

import { isCanonicalizable } from "../../emit/opId.js";
import type { GateCatalog } from "../../ports/index.js";
import type { ValidationFinding } from "../errors.js";
import type {
  DeclPath,
  EqualsRuleDecl,
  MembershipRule,
  MessageTemplate,
  NodeDecl,
  Selector,
  SurfaceDecl,
} from "./vocabulary.js";

/**
 * ADR-019 D1: THE ENGINE — one validator over declaration DATA, running
 * the SAME declaration on the file-walk and direct-construction channels.
 *
 * The engine consumes a `SurfaceDecl`; it knows the VOCABULARY and knows
 * nothing about any particular rule. A per-rule branch here (a literal
 * `if (rule === X)`) is ADR-019 D9 tripwire #2 — a STOP and a scope
 * review, never a work-around. Where two measured lanes disagree on a
 * grain (path address, lane order, gating), the disagreement is DECLARED
 * (`at:`, `laneOrder:`, `gating:`), which is the same instrument the
 * audit's §5 F3 identified for path grain.
 *
 * Channel symmetry is structural, not argued (D1): attributes that read
 * SOURCE text (`sourceForm`, the deep key-stringness scan) are declared
 * once with `channel: "file"`; the engine runs them where a source exists
 * and skips them where none does.
 *
 * This module VALIDATES and applies plain `default:` materialization. It
 * does NOT derive values — derivation is the separately-named normalizer
 * (D3, `normalizer.ts`).
 */

/** The channel the declaration is being run on. */
export type EngineChannel =
  | { readonly kind: "file"; readonly doc: Document; readonly source: string }
  | { readonly kind: "direct" };

export interface EngineOptions {
  readonly channel: EngineChannel;

codex
### F13 — A shared id-grammar új alkalmazási helye csendben elveszhet egy string elírásával

CLASS: future-trap  
LENS:  5  
WHAT: A ch8-C10 közös id-grammar hatókörének bővítésekor a maintainernek a contract-sort, a `vc-id-class` deklarációt/referenciát, a vocabulary jelentését és a file/direct negatív fixture-öket kell együtt követnie, de egy elírt `valueClass` név typecheck-green és az engine finding nélkül elfogadja az értéket.  
WHERE: `v3/implementation/contracts/ch8-template-format-contract.md:193`; `v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts:28-40` és a `valueClass: "idClass"` site-ok; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:571-583`; `v3/src/definition/validate.test.ts:242-389`  
EVIDENCE: A jelenlegi rule-t a `vc-id-class` grammar hordozza, öt site hivatkozza, a V5 fixture-grid pedig a négy id-osztály × space/dot/empty eseteket és NBSP-t ellenőrzi. Viszont `valueClass` típusa sima `string`, és az engine kódja ismeretlen névnél `return { ok: true, value: frame.value }`. `/tmp`-be fordított engine-en egy `valueClass:"TYPO"` declaration és `id:"!!!"` bemenet futtatásának valós kimenete `[]`. Egy jövőbeli új id-site pozitív fixture-rel tehát compile-, lint- és suite-green maradhat, miközben a grammar ott soha nem fut.  
WHY IT MATTERS: A deklaráció centralizálja a szabály szövegét, de nem zárja le a szabály elérését; a packet-author a deklarációból önmagában nem tudja bizonyítani, hogy minden név szerinti referencia feloldódik, így a format-growth legvalószínűbb karbantartási hibája néma alulvalidálás.
codex
### F14 — A packet-author a deklarációból nem tudja a default finding-grain-t

CLASS: future-trap  
LENS:  2b  
WHAT: Egy új required fixed-map mező task-projekciójánál a `presence: {required:true}` deklaráció sem önmagában, sem a vocabulary kommentjéből nem mondja meg, hogy az elhagyott `at` container-pathot jelent; ezt csak az engine implementációja árulja el.  
WHERE: `v3/src/definition/schema/vocabulary.ts:123-138`; `v3/src/definition/schema/engine.ts:628-645`; `v3/src/definition/schema/templateFormat.ts` valamennyi rövid `presence: { required: true }` site-ja  
EVIDENCE: A `presence.at` opcionális `"container" | "self"`, de a vocabulary nem deklarál explicit defaultot. Az engine a `presence.at === "self" ? child : parent` kifejezéssel minden más esetet, így az `undefined`-ot is containerre visz. Konkrétan egy packet-author új required mezőt projektál, csak verdict-fixture-t ír, és a declaration alapján field-path findingot dokumentál; a kód és a suite zöld lehet, miközben a tényleges path a container. A teljes garanciához ezért legalább a contract/tag → `templateFormat.ts` → `vocabulary.ts` → `engine.ts` láncot kell olvasnia, normalizer/residual érintettségnél még további fájlokat is.  
WHY IT MATTERS: A deklaráció nincs önmagában elég közel egy implementálható task-contracthoz; a következő packet-fázis újra az interpreter byte-jaiból rekonstruál implicit jelentést, éppen ott, ahol a finding-path paritás az ADR szerint load-bearing.
codex
A vizsgálatot lezártam; minden kért lenshez van konkrét eredmény, további mélyfúrást nem indítok. Most csak a HEAD-et és a byte-érintetlenséget ellenőrzöm, majd a kért rövid zárást adom.

HEAD: `889ffa66b569178ab0f039970338a5c27797b72f`

Osztályonként: **4 design-error / 2 over-promise / 4 future-trap / 4 confirmed-sound**

Lenses: **mind teljesítve** — 1a–1d, 2a–2c, 3, 4a–4c, 5. A repository változatlan maradt.

**Is the approach sound?** Igen. Az egy deklaratív hatóság, egy közös interpreter, külön normalizer és név szerint korlátozott residual helyes irány. Ennek feltétele, hogy a deklarációt valódi nyelvként kezeljék: a `channel` és path szemantikája legyen koherens és fail-closed, minden authority-rész — a substrate is — legyen taggel címezhető, a live schema byte-lockja lépjen életbe minden további módosítás előtt, és a residual-taxonómia őszintén számolja a boundary miatt kézzel maradó szabályokat. Ezek nélkül a megvalósítás többet ígér, mint amennyit garantál, de maga az irány nem szorul lecserélésre.

Ennek feltétele, hogy a deklarációt valódi nyelvként kezeljék: a `channel` és path szemantikája legyen koherens és fail-closed, minden authority-rész — a substrate is — legyen taggel címezhető, a live schema byte-lockja lépjen életbe minden további módosítás előtt, és a residual-taxonómia őszintén számolja a boundary miatt kézzel maradó szabályokat.
